The window carried 216 announcements from 106 companies. 60 of them are in COMPANIES MASTER (23 T groups) and 46 are not (8 N groups). 187 PDFs downloaded; they hold 143 unique files and 2,332 pages counting repeat copies, 477 of them scanned pages read as images. 7 items had no PDF (rating updates and insider trades). 22 items failed with HTTP 403, all on the NSE SME / Emerge link, and were read from the Screener summary only: Cellecor Gadgets, Chandan Healthca, FlySBS Aviation, Karnika Industri, Macpower CNC (2), Silkflex Polymer, Taurian MPS, Tirupati Forge (3), Unihealth Hosp (2), Urban Enviro (2), Vivid Electromech, Womancart (3) and Zota Health Care (3; the N03 analyst read Zota's press release from the NSE archive instead). Duplicate postings were heavy: Hexaware posted one file under 9 items, Route Mobile and Sasken under 7 each, Aarti Pharma, Aeroflex and Onemi under 4 each. Aarti Pharma also re-uploaded a "clear version" of its results beside the original, with the same numbers. Premier Polyfilm item 131 is a blank signature-only PDF. No wrong file sat behind any link: a fresh Notion check of 33 master companies gave 33 MATCH, 0 MISMATCH. Screener summaries mislabel three periods: R Systems (says Q2 FY2026, it is Q3 CY26), Hexaware (says Q2 FY26/FY27, it is Q3 CY26) and Womancart (says Q2/H1 FY26). Prices for all 106 companies were refreshed from screener.in between 05:35 and 05:40 IST on 29-Oct, before most of the day's results were traded.

## Route Mobile: margin line cleared by ₹0.45 Cr, cash went to a vendor

Ten filings, but they cover only four documents. Items 150 to 156 are the same 18-page board outcome filed seven times under different headings (identical text). The other three are the press release (148), the investor deck (149) and the concall audio link (147). The audio is not transcribed, so anything said only on the call is not covered here.

Status on file: HELD, "HOLD 4%". Avg cost ₹509. Entry Max ₹628 and MoS ₹502 in the page properties. The 11-Aug AR review moved these to ₹463 / ₹579 in the page body, but did not update the properties. Add ≤₹510, hard floor ₹450, trim at >₹820 (1%) and >₹1,330 (50%) (Notion, Position Size). Your Notion page set one decisive metric for this print: consolidated Adjusted EBITDA margin. At ≥10%, Trigger 1 is retired. Below 10% for a second quarter in a row, Trigger 1 fires and a 50% exit is mandatory (Notion, Q1 FY27 review Step 8; AR review Step 13).

### The decisive metric: 10.04%, and only because of one add-back

[FILED] The deck puts Q2 Adjusted EBITDA at ₹1,184.2 mn on revenue of ₹11,797 mn. That is 10.0% (deck p.17, p.19). To two decimals it is 10.04% (my arithmetic, not filed). At exactly 10% the figure would be ₹1,179.7 mn. So the line was cleared by ₹4.5 mn, about ₹0.45 Cr.

Now the bridge on p.19:
- Reported EBITDA ₹1,150.2 mn = 9.75% (my arithmetic). This is the second quarter in a row below 10% on a reported basis. Q1 was ₹1,054.8 mn = 9.16%, which matches the Notion log.
- Add-back: "Professional fees related to Masivian data breach remediation" ₹69.0 mn in Q2, against ₹13.6 mn in Q1.
- Deduction: capitalised intangibles under development ₹35.0 mn. This one is conservative and fine.
- Without the remediation add-back, Q2 Adjusted EBITDA is ₹1,115.2 mn = 9.45% (my arithmetic, not filed).

Gate test, Trigger 1 ("EBITDA margin <10% for 2 consecutive quarters"):
- On the metric as defined on file (management's Adjusted EBITDA): passes at 10.04%. Trigger 1 is retired, but with no room to spare.
- On reported EBITDA (the original thesis says "EBITDA margin", with no "adjusted"): 9.75% after 9.16%. That is two quarters in a row, so it fires.
- On adjusted, but without accepting the remediation add-back: 9.45%. Fires.

The Q1 call called the Masivian incident "minor, provisions cover" (Notion, Q1 concall addendum, Prior Q6). Q2 remediation fees are five times Q1's, and H1 now totals ₹8.26 Cr (deck p.19). A cost that has run for two quarters and is growing is hard to call one-off. Please rule on which reading Trigger 1 uses, and whether the remediation add-back counts. The whole quarter turns on this ₹6.9 Cr.

### Gross margin recovered into management's band, not to the gate

[FILED] Gross profit was ₹263.95 Cr on revenue of ₹1,179.67 Cr, a gross margin of 22.38% (results p.7; deck p.16 shows 22.4%). That compares with 20.88% in Q1 and 22.1% in Q2 FY26.
- Section 8 item 1 red line (<22% for 2 quarters): Q2 is above 22%, so the red sequence stops at one leg.
- Cap-5% gate (GM ≥23%): still fails.
- Management's own band (21.5–23%, Q1 call): delivered, inside the band. The band's ceiling is still below the 25% FY29 base assumption.

H1 gross margin is 21.64% (my arithmetic). The FY27 base column on file assumes 23.5%. That projection is not being met.

### Two cash and balance-sheet items: the ₹117 Cr vendor advance and the ₹6.9 Cr remediation

[FILED] H1 consolidated CFO was ₹33.34 Cr on PAT of ₹151.10 Cr, which is 0.22x (results p.10). The deck's own "reported CFO/EBITDA" is 15% (p.20). The deck then adds back ₹1,168.3 mn "Advance paid to vendor for services which will be availed in the subsequent period" to reach an "adjusted" 66%. Other current assets rose from ₹203.50 Cr to ₹364.23 Cr in six months, up ₹160.73 Cr (results p.9).

This is the same practice that produced the FY26 exceptional loss. The ₹107.96 Cr SIAC write-off was an advance "under a minimum guaranteed SMS volume agreement with a vendor" (results p.11, note 7a). The ₹27.91 Cr write-off was another vendor advance (note 7b). Raj Gill called vendor prepayments a "deliberate strategy for commercial benefits" (Notion, Q4 FY26 concall). The Q1 FY27 watchpoint #5, "any new large vendor prepayments disclosed", now has its answer: ₹116.83 Cr. That is about 59% of the original ₹196.61 Cr SIAC advance (my arithmetic). The filing names neither the vendor, the tenor nor any guarantee.

Two readings:
- Bull: the advance buys cheaper termination and "enhanced routing" capacity. The deck credits that routing for the 150 bps QoQ gross margin recovery (p.16). If it pays back as cheaper traffic over H2, it is working capital timing, not risk.
- Bear: the gross margin recovery was partly bought with balance-sheet risk, through the same instrument that cost ₹136 Cr last year. Days payable also fell from 64 to 50 (deck p.21), so the company is now funding its suppliers on two fronts.
- The observation that separates them: other current assets at 31-Mar-2027. If they fall back below about ₹250 Cr, gross margin holds at 22% or above, and H2 CFO/EBITDA reaches the promised 75% or above, the bull case is right. If the balance stays above ₹300 Cr, or any part of it is provisioned, the bear case is right.

Management's Q1 promise of "full-year cash conversion 75–100% of EBITDA" is behind at H1: 15% reported, 66% even on their own adjusted basis.

Trailing four-quarter CFO/PAT is still about 1.12x: H2 FY26 CFO ₹378.28 Cr plus H1 FY27 CFO ₹33.34 Cr, against trailing PAT of ₹368.09 Cr (my arithmetic, not filed). So the item 3 red line (<0.5x) does not fire. The H1 alone is the weakest half since FY24.

Standalone is worse. Parent CFO was −₹126.69 Cr. Parent receivables rose from ₹241.36 Cr to ₹391.25 Cr, which is about 157 days of annualised H1 revenue, against about 114 days at March (results p.15–16; my arithmetic). Consolidated DSO is about 74 days, so the stretch sits in India.

### The rest of the monitoring list, tested

- New Products revenue: ₹128.4 Cr, up 36.7% YoY and 35.8% QoQ (deck p.10). Item 5 green on its face. But Heltar was consolidated from 29-Jul, and the deck does not separate organic from acquired revenue. The jump of ₹33.9 Cr QoQ is partly inorganic. The share Heltar contributed is not disclosed.
- Heltar (item 14): consideration is now disclosed at ₹33.66 Cr: ₹23.88 Cr paid at closing and ₹9.78 Cr deferred for 18 months (results p.11 note 6; deck p.9). That is below the ₹40–150 Cr working assumption on file. The core team is retained ("development led by the Heltar core team", deck p.9). There is no revenue KPI. By the item's own definition this is AMBER.
- M&A pace (item 15): ₹33.66 Cr cumulative for FY27 against the ₹300 Cr line. GREEN. Goodwill rose from ₹545.68 Cr to ₹573.85 Cr (results p.9). The filing does not split this between Heltar and FX.
- Truecaller BM (item 13): absent from the press release and the deck. The clock was at 2 of 3 (AR review). On the written record this is 3 of 3, which is RED by the item's definition, "3 quarters silent from CEO commentary". It is provisional until the call audio is checked.
- Salesforce AgentExchange (item 16): named on deck p.7 with no revenue figure. Clock 1 of 4.
- Proximus revenue KPI (Strategic Premium expiry: "Q2 FY27 concall must deliver at least one Proximus revenue KPI or drops to +1.0x"): there is none in the press release or the deck. The deck shows only a group award and "Proximus Global sales teams" as a distribution channel (p.7, p.9). Unless the call gave a ₹ figure, the premium falls from +1.25x to +1.0x on your own rule.
- Concentration (item 6): Top-10 is 42%, against 43% at FY26 (deck p.13). GREEN. Clients above $1 mn fell from 80 to 73 (annualised), and above $10 mn from 9 to 8. The tail is thinning. Customer A (15.1% at FY26) is not updated.
- Volume promise (Q1 call, "10–15% next couple of quarters"): Q2 billable transactions were 47.6 bn against 45.2 bn, up 5.3% YoY (deck p.16). H1 was up 10.7%. Q2 alone missed.
- India segment: result ₹27.11 Cr against −₹4.00 Cr in Q1, on revenue of ₹302.22 Cr against ₹236.21 Cr (results p.8). This fits the "large bank customer restored in Q2" promise [INFERENCE]. Standalone PAT was ₹33.13 Cr against ₹16.16 Cr. Prior Q3 (India loss: structural or one-off) leans one-off.
- PAT quality: PAT ₹82.55 Cr, up 20.4% QoQ. The effective tax rate fell to 18.2% from 25.1%. At Q1's rate, PAT would have been about ₹75.6 Cr, so roughly ₹6.9 Cr of the QoQ gain is tax (my arithmetic). Employee cost was ₹90.81 Cr, up 17% QoQ and 27% YoY. Part of that is Heltar's team.
- Cash (item 10): cash, bank balances and current investments total ₹1,357.56 Cr, with zero borrowings (results p.9). The deck shows ₹13,556 mn. GREEN, but down about ₹48 Cr from March on the same definition (my arithmetic).
- Audit scope: MSKA's first limited review. It did not review 24 subsidiaries with H1 PAT of ₹89.62 Cr before consolidation adjustments, against consolidated PAT of ₹151.10 Cr (results p.4). Q1 logged 35.3% of PAT as resting on component auditors. This is not like-for-like, but the direction is up.

### Two board items worth reading

IPO object rescission (item 151). [FILED] On 7-May-2026 the board approved varying the use of the unspent ₹65 Cr IPO "purchase of office premises in Mumbai" object. It has now rescinded that, so the money stays for the office purchase (results p.2). The money has been parked for six years. This answers Prior Q9 from the Q1 review. [INFERENCE] A change of IPO objects needs a special resolution, and your AR review logged that the AGM notice carried zero special resolutions. So the May variation was never taken to shareholders, and the rescission closes a loose end rather than signalling a new plan. What is missing: a timeline, and whether the premises might come from a related party.

MOA alteration (item 150). New main objects cover AI, GenAI, ML, "Digital Identity solutions", cloud and CPaaS (results p.18). Shareholder approval is needed. Digital identity is Telesign's business inside Proximus Global. Two readings:
- Housekeeping, so that Heltar and the Salesforce app sit inside the objects.
- Groundwork for moving Proximus identity business into, or through, Route. That would bring large related-party transactions with the parent. It is also the opposite of a strategic review (Trigger 2).

The postal ballot notice will show which. Watch it for related-party caps, which the AGM notably did not seek.

Smaller points:
- ESOP lapses of 2,860, against 22,000 in Q1. Attrition has eased.
- ₹4 second interim dividend, record date 2-Nov. FY27 is running at ₹16 annualised, against the ₹16.5 promise.
- The trading window opens 30-Oct. Any insider buying at these prices would be information.

### Price and levels

₹435 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:39 IST). 52-week range ₹407–771. Market cap ₹2,741 Cr.
- Below the ₹450 hard floor (Notion, Position Size). The floor descends from the Devil's Advocate rule "if breached, exit 50% regardless of narrative" (₹420, raised to ₹450 at Q4 FY26).
- Below the ₹510 add line, both MoS figures (₹502 property, ₹463 AR body) and your ₹509 cost (−14.5%).
- Notion CMP of ₹526 is from 28-Apr; it is stale.

Net cash is about ₹215 a share, 49.5% of market cap. Trailing PAT of ₹368 Cr puts the stock at about 7.4x, and about 3.8x after taking out cash (my arithmetic).

The rules now point both ways. The tightened add rule ("price ≤₹510 AND Q2 Adj EBITDA ≥10%") is technically met. The hard floor says exit 50%. Please rule which governs. My read is that neither should run on autopilot until the Trigger 1 ruling above is made.

### Fair value consequence

H1 revenue of ₹2,331 Cr annualises to about ₹4,662 Cr. That sits on the FY27 bear column (₹4,648 Cr), not base (₹4,958 Cr). H1 Adjusted EBITDA margin of 9.8% is below even the FY29 bear margin of 11% (Notion, Section 2). H1 EPS of ₹22.57 runs at about ₹45 a year, against the ₹62.6 FY27 base (my arithmetic).

If the Strategic Premium drops to +1.0x, the destination PE goes from about 18.0x to about 17.75x. The bigger issue is the 35% base weight in the ₹1,131 PW fair value: H1 is tracking bear on both revenue and margin. That is my read, not a re-run of the model.

### Verdict

No thesis-broken trigger fires on the definition on file. Trigger 1 is retired by ₹0.45 Cr, but only if the ₹6.9 Cr remediation add-back is accepted. It fires on reported EBITDA. The live new risk is the ₹116.83 Cr vendor advance: it is the trigger-5 instrument, rebuilt in six months. Read together, the print moves the name further toward the weakened end, not toward an add, even with price below the add line. The deferred 4%→3% trim and the ₹450 floor breach are both live. That is your call.

## PNGS Gargi FJ: H1 cash passes 0.35x only on supplier credit

Gargi is in COMPANIES MASTER as HELD, Position Size "Large 10% (FRAMEWORK OVERRIDE)", Avg Cost ₹884, Entry Max ₹1,108, MoS ₹886, Promoter Verdict TRUSTWORTHY-with-flags (Notion, properties). Exit lines on file: weekly close below ₹680 (trigger 5), stop ₹650, review on a monthly close below ₹780, trims on monthly closes above ₹1,400 and ₹1,800 (Notion, Quick Reference, Trim Ladder). Two tracker rows are linked: "Gargi H1 CFO/PAT from Reg 33 cash flow" (Next Check 15-Nov, Signal Health Threatens) and "Gargi Q3FY27 revenue ≥₹57.03 Cr" (Next Check 08-Jan-2027) (Notion, tracker pages). Four rulings are open on the page: the trigger 1 reading, the actual price and position against ₹680 / ₹650, the 10% to 5% trim live since 1-Aug, and whether the parent's just-in-time change is an RPT restructuring under trigger 4 (Notion, 22-Oct Rulings pending).

Three filings: the board outcome and the results (identical files), and the Q2/H1 investor deck.

### What it says, in numbers [FILED]

All ₹ Cr, converted from lakhs (PNGS_Gargi_FJ_Unaudited_Financial_Results_For_Q2_Of_FY_5b16be.pdf, p.3-5):

| Line | Q2FY27 | Q2FY26 | H1FY27 | H1FY26 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Revenue from operations | 54.00 | 46.45 | 84.21 | 73.76 |
| Other income | 1.57 | 1.28 | 3.45 | 2.49 |
| PAT | 10.72 | 10.27 | 15.77 | 15.59 |
| PAT margin | 19.85% | 22.12% | 18.73% | 21.13% |
| Operating EBITDA margin (ex other income) | 26.1% | 28.0% | 23.8% | 26.2% |
| Gross margin | 40.6% | 40.5% | 41.2% | 40.5% |

Margins are from the deck (PNGS_Gargi_FJ_Announcement_under_Regulation_30_LODR-In_dcde06.pdf, p.9) and match my arithmetic on the results. The OCR of the results prints H1 revenue as 8,121.41 lakhs; Q1 + Q2 and total income both give 8,421.41, which the deck confirms (₹842.1 Mn).

Cash flow for H1 (results p.5): CFO ₹6.93 Cr. Inside it, trade payables rose ₹26.92 Cr, inventory rose ₹25.71 Cr, receivables rose ₹16.14 Cr and other current assets rose ₹2.80 Cr. Balance sheet (p.4): inventory ₹73.95 Cr vs ₹48.24 Cr at Mar-26; receivables ₹29.77 Cr vs ₹13.63 Cr; trade payables ₹33.17 Cr vs ₹6.25 Cr; new short-term borrowings ₹0.17 Cr; cash ₹0.18 Cr plus other bank balances ₹77.92 Cr.

Costs grew faster than sales in Q2: employee cost +50% (₹1.11 Cr vs ₹0.74 Cr), depreciation +142% (₹0.89 Cr vs ₹0.37 Cr), finance cost +94%, other expenses +33%, against revenue +16% (p.3; my arithmetic).

Notes (p.6-7): 141 POS on 28-Oct (36 parent SIS, 51 other SIS, 54 EBO including kiosks). Preferential proceeds used ₹8.32 Cr of ₹10.91 Cr. Management writes that 30 EBOs and sales points opened over the past 6 to 18 months, and the company "is yet to generate a sizable impact on revenue" [FILED, management note]. H1FY26 included nine Navratri days; Navratri started 10-Oct this year. No analyst call until "after Q3 or Q4".

### The gates, leg by leg

- **Trigger 2, PAT margin below 15% for 2 quarters: passes.** Q2 19.85%, and other income was only 10.9% of PBT (₹1.57 Cr of ₹14.39 Cr; my arithmetic). Unlike Q1's 16.71%, this one does not lean on other income.
- **Trigger 1, growth below 20% for 2 quarters: the filed figure confirms the update.** Q2 revenue ₹54.00 Cr ties to the 7-Oct update's ₹53.98 Cr (Notion, 08-Oct entry), so it reads +16.25% (my arithmetic). On the reported-YoY reading you used on 1-Aug, the trigger has fired. On the festive-adjusted reading it still waits for Q3 at ₹57.03 Cr or more. The results now put the Navratri shift in a filed note, but the gate wording still says nothing about calendars. The ruling is still yours.
- **H1 CFO/PAT, the "single cleanest metric" (Notion, Q1 review): 0.439x.** That is ₹6.93 Cr over ₹15.77 Cr (my arithmetic). It sits between your bear line (below 0.35x) and the re-add leg (0.50x or more). It is better than FY26's 0.352x.
- **But the cash came from suppliers.** Payables went from ₹6.25 Cr to ₹33.17 Cr. Without that ₹26.92 Cr, H1 CFO would be about minus ₹20.0 Cr (my arithmetic, not filed). Payable days on H1 purchases are about 81, against about 22 for FY26 (₹33.17 Cr / ₹75.25 Cr × 183; ₹6.25 Cr / ₹101.74 Cr × 365; my arithmetic). The filing does not say who the supplier is. If it is P N Gadgil & Sons, then longer credit from the parent is a change in related-party terms. That links to the open trigger 4 question [INFERENCE]. Please rule on whether a payables-funded CFO counts toward the 0.50x line.
- **Trigger 3 (FY27 full-year CFO/PAT below 0.50x): not testable until FY27 ends.** H1 at 0.44x leaves H2 needing better than 0.50x, while the payables build has to unwind or be funded [INFERENCE].
- **Inventory days: worse.** About 273 days on H1 cost of goods (₹73.95 Cr / ₹49.54 Cr × 183), against 206.5 at FY26 and the 150-170 you want (Notion, Q1 review; my arithmetic). A pre-Navratri stock-up explains part of it, since the festival started ten days after the balance sheet date [INFERENCE].
- **Receivable days (monitoring item 8): RED.** About 65 days (₹29.77 Cr / ₹84.21 Cr × 183) against a red line above 60 and 33 at FY26 (Notion, Section 8 and Q1 review; my arithmetic).
- **The JIT test from the 08-Oct review fails on half of it.** That review said that if the parent's just-in-time change is real, inventory should rise and receivables from the parent should fall (Notion, 08-Oct, point 6.2). Inventory rose. Receivables more than doubled. The JIT story does not explain the receivables [INFERENCE].
- **Store adds (item 5): Q2 failed.** The deck's chart has 136 POS at Q2: 36 parent SIS, 50 third-party SIS, 50 EBO. At FY26 it had 126: 35, 53, 38 (deck p.22). So third-party SIS fell by 3 in H1. That ends the "zero closures" line on file (Notion, FY26 deck item 7).

### The ₹718.9 Mn cash gap is closed

Mar-26 other bank balances are 7,189.64 lakhs, which is ₹718.96 Mn (results p.4). Mar-26 cash and cash equivalents are 85.55 lakhs, the same as the cash-flow closing figure (p.5). The deck's ₹727.5 Mn "cash" is those two lines added together (deck p.10). So the gap was a definitional difference: fixed deposits are left out of cash equivalents. It was not an error. FY26 CFO of ₹11.03 Cr stands (p.5). Question 1 of the 17 can come off the list.

### What the deck contradicts

- **EBO share.** The deck gives 21.44% for H1FY27 (p.5). The 7-Oct business update gave 18.30% for the same half (Notion, 08-Oct entry). At 21.44%, H1 EBO sales are ₹18.06 Cr and Q2 non-EBO is ₹42.84 Cr, +7.2% YoY, not the +13.8% logged on 08-Oct (my arithmetic, using the logged Q1 EBO ₹6.90 Cr and H1FY26 base 12.06%). The "non-EBO stabilisation passes" call depends on which number is right.
- **Product mix.** The deck gives silver about 37%, 14KT diamond about 57% and 9KT gold about 3% for Q2 (p.19). The Q1 deck gave silver 67%, diamond 22% and gold 6% (Notion, Q1 review Q15). FY26 diamond was about 34% (Notion, FY26 deck). A 35-point swing in one quarter is either a big mix change or a change in how mix is measured. Gross margin did not move (40.6% vs 40.5%).
- **Store arithmetic.** "6 POS added post Q2FY27" plus 136 makes 142, not the 141 shown (p.5). "15 POS already opened till date" (p.15) equals the net gain from 126 to 141. That means no closures, yet third-party SIS fell by 3.
- **Guidance.** The "FY27 & Beyond" slide no longer shows "~35% Revenue CAGR" (p.15), which it did in the FY26 and Q1 decks (Notion, FY26 deck item 9). It now says "FY 2028 Target to improve store economics and monetize newly opened POS". The silence is the guidance cut, now in a filed document.
- **Governance claims.** The deck still claims "regular quarterly disclosures and investor concalls" (p.16), while the results say there will be no call until after Q3 or Q4 (results p.7). It still uses the parent's "no store closures" claim (p.17), which was flagged on 1-Aug (FN08). It describes the balance sheet as "debt-free" (p.15), with ₹0.17 Cr of borrowings on it.
- **Discounting into the festive season.** Campaign creatives offer flat 25% off 925 silver and up to 100% off making charges on diamond jewellery, valid till 15-Nov-2026, at all P N Gadgil & Sons showrooms (deck p.27, image). Q3 gross margin will carry this [INFERENCE].

### Two readings

- Bull: Q2 PAT margin 19.85% with no festive days and normal other income. CFO turned positive at 0.44x. Gross margin held. 141 POS and 15 adds put the 20-25 store guide within reach. Inventory was stocked for a festival that started ten days after the period ended.
- Bear: H1 PAT +1.2% on revenue +14%. Inventory days 273, receivable days 65, and CFO only positive because suppliers funded ₹26.9 Cr. Management itself writes that the 30 new outlets have not moved revenue. Store monetisation has moved to FY28. The deck contradicts the business update on EBO share.
- What separates them: the Q3 business update (early January 2027, tracker on file) at ₹57.03 Cr or more. Then the FY27 cash flow (about May 2027), showing whether payables unwind against inventory that sold. Add one condition to the tracker: Q3 should confirm the EBO share on one basis.

### Fair value

Your 08-Oct base case is FY27 revenue ₹179 Cr and PAT ₹30 Cr, at 17% margin (Notion, 08-Oct projections). H1 PAT of ₹15.77 Cr is 53% of that, so the PAT line is on track. H2 revenue must reach ₹94.79 Cr, +25.3% on H2FY26's ₹75.64 Cr (my arithmetic). The ₹2,070 base FV (Notion, Quick Reference) rests on 32% growth and is not on any live path. The 08-Oct base FY29 EPS of ₹48 at your 30.6x is about ₹1,469 (my arithmetic, not a logged FV).

### Price

₹515 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:38 IST). 52-week range ₹494-1,197; market cap ₹539 Cr. The deck prints the same ₹515 for 28-Oct (p.28). My arithmetic:

- 24.3% below the ₹680 weekly-close exit and 20.8% below the ₹650 stop.
- 34.0% below the ₹780 review line.
- 41.7% below the ₹884 avg cost.
- 10.3% below the ₹574 on file (Notion CMP, 22-Oct), and 4.3% above the 52-week low.
- On TTM EPS of ₹30.09 (FY26 ₹30.05 less H1FY26 ₹15.02 plus H1FY27 ₹15.06, results p.3), that is 17.1x.

### Verdict

No new thesis-broken trigger fires on this print. Trigger 2 passes cleanly. Trigger 3 cannot be tested until year end. Trigger 1 stands on the reported-YoY reading, now on filed numbers. The H1 cash test lands in the amber band, but only on supplier credit, so it does not meet the 0.50x re-add leg either way. Trigger 5, the price rule, has been below its line on every reading since late July and is lower again. The page still records no action on the exit or the trim. That is your call.

### Cross-links

- PNGS Reva (sister): its Navratri sales rose 35.01% (Notion, 22-Oct entry). Gargi has filed no festive update, and it is discounting at the parent's showrooms until 15-Nov.
- P N Gadgil Jewellers (separate listed company): its Silvostyle acquisition puts a fashion-silver competitor in about 80-105 stores (Notion, 08-Oct review).
- Fabtech (this batch): another name where supplier credit or working capital, not earnings, sets the cash read.

## Macfos: master gate fails, cash went the wrong way

The H1 filing is the master decision gate your page has pointed to since April. The P&L is the best quarter on record. The balance sheet and cash flow fail every cash test on file.

Items read: 91 (risk committee, 2 pages), 92 (results outcome, 15 pages, 14 image pages) and 93 (board outcome, 16 pages, 14 image pages). Items 92 and 93 carry the same results pack; all 14 image pages are byte-identical, so I read them once, every page. Item 93's cover letter (pp.1-2) is the only extra text.

What it says [FILED], standalone, ₹ Cr (filed in ₹ lakh):

| Line | Q2 FY27 | Q1 FY27 | Q2 FY26 | H1 FY27 | H1 FY26 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Revenue from operations | 98.73 | 81.34 | 68.48 | 180.06 | 127.75 |
| Other income | 0.78 | 1.12 | 0.79 | 1.90 | 1.39 |
| Change in inventory | (18.46) | (17.91) | (10.51) | (36.37) | (15.74) |
| Finance cost | 1.50 | 1.00 | 0.86 | 2.50 | 1.42 |
| PAT | 7.36 | 5.82 | 5.08 | 13.18 | 10.04 |

Source: standalone P&L (Macfos_Outcome_..._e9b75d.pdf, p.5). Consolidated PAT is ₹7.35 Cr for Q2 and ₹13.17 Cr for H1 (p.11). Subsidiaries remain trivial: Nuo Zhan HK total assets ₹1.01 lakh, nil revenue, not reviewed by any auditor; Macfos Electronics total assets ₹11.40 lakh, ₹1.16 lakh H1 revenue (pp.8-9).

### The gate, leg by leg

The master gate on file: "Bull ≥0.50x with inventory days ≤90; bear still negative (or <0.30x) with inventory days >90" (Notion, Q1 review Step 8; tracker falsifier).

- H1 CFO: ₹(21.85) Cr against H1 PAT ₹13.18 Cr. That is -1.66x (cash flow p.6; my arithmetic, not filed). Bear leg fires. It is worse than FY26's -0.31x and worse than the monitoring checklist's red line of "still deeply negative (< -0.5x)".
- Inventory: ₹79.57 Cr to ₹115.94 Cr in six months, up ₹36.37 Cr or 45.7% (balance sheet p.4). On your FY26 revenue basis (94.06 days), Sep-26 is about 118 days on annualised H1 revenue (my arithmetic, not filed). Bear leg fires.
- Your 22-Oct note set the size test: for H1 CFO to reach 0.5x, "Q2 has to release much of the Q1 build". Q2 did the opposite. It built ₹18.46 Cr, more than Q1's ₹17.91 Cr. The H1 build is 2.76x H1 PAT (my arithmetic).
- ST borrowings: ₹35.38 Cr to ₹63.12 Cr (p.4). The red line is "exceeds ₹50 Cr without revenue acceleration". The amount leg fails. The qualifier depends on reading: revenue did accelerate, from +37.2% YoY in Q1 to +44.2% in Q2 (my arithmetic). On a literal reading the carve-out saves it. On the intent of the line (debt funding stock, not sales), it is red. Please rule.
- Receivable days: about 15.4 on annualised H1 revenue, against FY26 15.66 and the 20-day red (my arithmetic). Pass.

Net debt excluding leases moved from about ₹34.1 Cr to about ₹62.0 Cr (borrowings ₹63.46 Cr less cash ₹1.51 Cr; my arithmetic). ST borrowings of ₹63.12 Cr now exceed the ₹59.8 Cr of ICICI plus DBS lines logged in the FY26 AR review (Notion, AR Step 4). So either limits were raised or there is a new lender. The filing does not say.

### The P&L is genuinely good

- Revenue +44.2% YoY in Q2, +21.4% QoQ. H1 +41.0% (my arithmetic). Green against ≥30%.
- Other income was flat at ₹0.78 Cr against ₹0.79 Cr. So the Q1 problem (60% of PAT growth from other income) is gone. Core PBT ex other income rose 52.0% (my arithmetic).
- Gross margin 24.05% against 22.79% a year ago, +126 bps (my arithmetic). Some mix lift is finally visible.
- Operating EBITDA margin 11.68% against 11.16% a year ago and 10.62% in Q1 (my arithmetic). Green against ≥10%.
- PAT margin 7.46% in Q2, 7.32% for H1. Just under the 7.5% green, above the 6.5% red. Amber.
- New P&L line: "Cost of material consumed" ₹0.33 Cr in Q2, ₹0.61 Cr in H1 (p.5). Small, but a trader is now consuming material. It fits the Macfos Electronics assembly question on file (F15-01).

### Two readings

Bull:
- Revenue accelerated and the margin gain is core, not treasury.
- Q4 is the seasonal peak (B2B and B2G year-end flush, Notion Q4 concall item 1). Stocking 1.06 lakh plus SKUs ahead of H2 can be a choice.
- Receivables did not stretch. Customers are paying.

Bear:
- Inventory grew faster than revenue: +45.7% in six months against +41% YoY sales. Inventory per rupee of sales is rising, not stable. The "if growth stopped tomorrow" test in the AR review called the build growth-induced. This half weakens that.
- Debt is funding stock, and finance cost rose 74.6% YoY and 49.0% QoQ (my arithmetic).
- Cumulative cash keeps falling. FY25, FY26 and H1 FY27 CFO sum to ₹(52.60) Cr against PAT of ₹56.74 Cr, -0.93x over 2.5 years (my arithmetic, mixed IGAAP and Ind AS bases). The thesis-broken trigger (cumulative CFO/PAT below 0.30x by FY28) is not due, but the hole it must climb out of got deeper.

The observation that separates them: FY27 audited cash flow (about Apr-2027). H2 FY27 CFO must be positive by at least ₹21.85 Cr to bring FY27 CFO to zero, and inventory days must return to 90 or below at 31-Mar-2027. An earlier read: Q3 finance cost (results about Feb-2027). Above ₹1.50 Cr means borrowings kept climbing.

### Fair value consequence

The tracker row says a structural reading moves the cash multiplier toward 0.65x (Notion, rating tracker falsifier). On the AR-revised base (ROCE base 21x), 21 x 0.65 + 4 = 17.65x against 20.8x today (my arithmetic). The base FY29 EPS ₹52.93 at 17.65x is about ₹934 on the P/E method alone, against ₹1,111 base on file (my arithmetic, illustrative, not a re-run). The 25% CAGR entry would fall from ₹569 to about ₹478. I do not say the multiplier must move. I say this filing is the evidence the page said would decide it.

ROCE: a rough H1 annualised EBIT over average capital employed gives about 25.7%, and about 22.9% on closing capital (my arithmetic, not Screener basis). The AR sensitivity says ROCE below 25% takes the ROCE base to 19x. Borderline. Screener's FY27 figure will settle it.

Projections: P&L is on base. H1 revenue ₹180 Cr against base FY27 ₹405 Cr; H1 PAT ₹13.18 Cr against base ₹32 Cr needs H2 ₹18.8 Cr, +21% on H2 FY26's ₹15.56 Cr (my arithmetic). Cash is not in any projection, and that is the problem.

Convert-to-invest: revenue ≥35% met; PAT margin ≥9% not met (7.32%); CFO/PAT ≥0.70x not met (-1.66x); Robu 2.0 share still undisclosed (single segment, note 3, p.13). One of four on H1, down from the 1.5 logged.

### Mainboard red line

The board outcome lists nine agenda items (item 93, pp.1-2). None is migration. On the 30-Sep reading the line had already fired. On the Q2-results reading it ran out on 28-Oct with no announcement. It has now fired on both readings. The ruling on which date applied no longer changes the outcome, only the date to log.

The risk committee (item 91) is the one thing that cuts the other way. The board formed it "voluntarily" under Reg 21, which binds only the top 1,000 listed companies (p.1). Chair Binod Prasad (WTD and CFO), members Atul Dumbre (MD) and Ravi Kant Jagetiya (independent). Two of three are executives. Bull reading: governance scaffolding a company builds before it migrates [INFERENCE]. Bear reading: a committee is not an application. It does not close the red line.

### What it does not say, and what is odd

- No reason for the ₹36 Cr stock build, no ageing, no slow-moving percentage. The deck may carry the slow-moving figure; none was filed.
- No disclosure of the enhanced bank limits behind ₹63 Cr of ST debt.
- Non-current trade receivables doubled, ₹0.29 Cr to ₹0.59 Cr (p.4). Small, but odd for an online seller.
- Capital advance of ₹1.00 Cr appears in other non-current assets (pp.4, 6). Purpose not given.
- The cash flow comparative is FY26 full year, labelled "As at March 31st, 2026", not H1 FY26 (p.6). The consolidated cash flow is titled "for the Quarter ended" and says "beginning of the period (April 01, 2025)" (p.12).
- FY26 CFO on the restated Ind AS basis is ₹(5.87) Cr (p.6), not the ₹(7.99) Cr IGAAP figure on the page. FY26 CFO/PAT becomes -0.23x, not -0.31x (my arithmetic). The difference is mostly finance cost and lease payments moving to financing. Same business, different number. The page should note which basis the FY28 trigger uses.
- Equity reconciliation shows "Difference (1)-(2)" as +84.44, but 9,512.21 less 9,596.65 is -84.44 (p.14, p.15). Sign slip.
- Three share counts in one filing: 1,03,58,503 (standalone, p.5), 1,03,58,504 (consolidated, p.11), 10,358,502 (note 6, p.13). Same slip as F14-01 in Q1.
- The cover letter again lists statutory dues, MSME delays and litigation "If any" with no value and no Nil (item 93, pp.1-2). Same as F13-02.
- Auditor: unmodified limited review, with the same Other Matter that comparatives are management-converted and not reviewed (pp.3, 8-9).

### Price

₹1,110 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:37 IST). 52-week range ₹655 to ₹1,315. Market cap ₹1,150 Cr. The filing was signed 17:07 IST on 28-Oct, after the close, so this price predates the cash flow [INFERENCE]. My arithmetic, not filed:

- Up 13.4% from ₹979 on file (Notion, 22-Oct).
- 11.2% above the ₹998 sleeve cost.
- 4.6% below the ₹1,164 first trim.
- 1.95x the ₹569 AVOID line; 1.86x the ₹596 property entry.
- 40.0x TTM EPS of ₹27.75 against a 21x destination.

### Verdict

The cash leg of the master gate fails outright. The thesis-broken trigger on cumulative CFO/PAT does not fire until FY28, but this is the observation the page said would show its direction, and it points down. The mainboard red line has now fired on either reading. On your rules, WATCHLIST with AVOID above ₹569 stands on fundamentals and moves toward the weaker end. The 3% sleeve is technical and has no stop on file. With price near the ₹1,164 trim and the fundamental gate failed, whether the sleeve should keep running on technical rules alone is your call.

## Forbes Precision: materials falsifier fires, inventory rebuilt, ₹60 Cr capex

You hold this name, with entry price and size still not recorded (Notion, Key Notes 16-Sep and 22-Oct). The lens is hold, trim or add.

Items 57, 58 and 59 are the same 8-page filing. The text and all six image pages are byte-identical across the three, so I read it once, every page.

What it says [FILED], ₹ Cr (filed in ₹ lakh):

| Line | Q2 FY27 | Q1 FY27 | Q2 FY26 | H1 FY27 | H1 FY26 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Revenue | 72.22 | 67.55 | 63.27 | 139.77 | 115.68 |
| Materials consumed | 34.86 | 23.48 | 24.48 | 58.34 | 42.30 |
| Change in inventories | (5.64) | 0.92 | (3.90) | (4.72) | (5.06) |
| Employee cost | 11.67 | 12.45 | 10.83 | 24.12 | 22.14 |
| Other expenses | 16.19 | 15.22 | 17.29 | 31.41 | 33.32 |
| PBT | 11.60 | 11.90 | 11.05 | 23.50 | 16.13 |
| PAT | 7.92 | 9.03 | 8.30 | 16.95 | 12.12 |

Source: P&L (Forbes_Precision_Capital_Expenditure_Proposal_bf1b0d.pdf, p.5). Limited review by Sharp & Tannan Associates LLP, unmodified (pp.3-4).

### The materials ratio, leg by leg

The Tier 1 row: falsified if "materials-plus-inventory-change stays at or above 36% of sales for two consecutive quarters while Western APT is flat or falling".

- Q1: (23.48 + 0.92) / 67.55 = 36.1%. Matches the row.
- Q2: (34.86 - 5.64) / 72.22 = 40.5%. Q2 FY26 was 32.5% (my arithmetic, not filed).
- Two consecutive quarters at or above 36%: fires.
- APT leg: logged as "plateaued since late Jul-2026" at about $300/kg (tracker row). I did not refresh APT. If that still holds, the row fires in full.

Your tracker note warned that pass-through was expected to drift toward 33-35%, not up. It went the other way by 440 bps in one quarter.

### Proof gate and margin

- Operating EBITDA ex other income: ₹15.14 Cr, 20.96% of revenue (my arithmetic). Q1 22.92%, Q2 FY26 23.04%.
- Proof gate (above 20% for two quarters beyond Q1): leg 1 passes by 96 bps. It is now one bad quarter from failing.
- Other expenses fell 6.4% YoY in rupees on 14.1% more revenue. Employee cost rose 7.8% YoY but fell 6.3% QoQ. The overhead discipline held. Materials took all of it and more.
- Tax rate jumped to 31.7% from 24.9% a year ago, with a ₹0.44 Cr deferred tax charge (my arithmetic). Q2 PAT fell 4.6% YoY even though PBT rose 5.0%. The filing gives no reason.
- Revenue growth slowed: +14.1% YoY in Q2 after +28.9% in Q1.

### Inventory, leg by leg

Two thresholds are on file and you were asked to rule which governs a ₹50-56 Cr print. The print is outside that band, so both answer the same way.

- Inventory ₹56.42 Cr to ₹66.32 Cr, up ₹9.90 Cr or 17.5% (balance sheet p.7). Days rose from about 82 to about 87 on annualised H1 revenue (my arithmetic).
- Key Notes test (≤₹50 Cr is firing): fails.
- Tracker falsifier (at or above ₹56 Cr with no improvement in days): fires.
- Raw material is not split out. The P&L shows FG and WIP up ₹4.72 Cr in H1, so the rest of the ₹9.90 Cr build, about ₹5.18 Cr, is raw material and stores (my arithmetic). On that derivation raw material is about ₹25 Cr, up from ₹19.9 Cr. The Key Notes rule says raw material "still ~₹20 Cr triggers structural reclassification and multiplier drops 1.00x to 0.65x". On the derived figure, that fires. It is my derivation, not a filed split.
- Q1's first destocking (+₹0.92 Cr) reversed in one quarter.

### Cash: conversion fine, the cushion is going

- H1 CFO ₹17.51 Cr against PAT ₹16.95 Cr: 1.03x (cash flow p.8; my arithmetic). Payables (+₹4.94 Cr) and receivables (-₹4.28 Cr) carried the inventory build.
- FCF about ₹13.73 Cr after ₹3.78 Cr capex. Dividend paid ₹25.25 Cr. The gap was funded by selling ₹15.68 Cr of mutual funds and ₹5.18 Cr of new long-term borrowing (p.8). That is the FY26 pattern on file ("a dividend that exceeded free cash flow"), repeated.
- Cash, bank and current investments fell from ₹29.51 Cr to ₹19.99 Cr. Borrowings rose from ₹14.87 Cr to ₹17.88 Cr. Net cash went from about ₹14.6 Cr to about ₹2.1 Cr (my arithmetic).
- Equity fell from ₹168.58 Cr to ₹159.00 Cr, because the dividend exceeded profit.

OR-5 asked whether the raw-material build is a permanent hedge or growth stock. H1 says it is still growing. That leans toward permanent.

### The ₹60 Cr capex

From the cover letter (p.1-2) [FILED]:
- ₹60 Cr to expand steel tap capacity from about 36 lakh to 46 lakh units a year, +27.8% (my arithmetic).
- Existing utilisation 80 to 92%.
- Added over 1 to 1.5 years. So output from about FY28 Q4 at the earliest.
- Funded by "internal accruals and debts". No split.

What it touches:
- This is the first capacity number in the record. It answers part of open item 1 ("₹46.7 Cr of capex and the plant did not get bigger"). It does not tie the ₹16 Cr gap.
- Pillar 3 growth-visibility failed on "capex-embedded growth 3.3% vs 15% test" (Key Notes 16-Sep). This capex adds 27.8% to steel taps. If steel taps are more than about 54% of revenue, embedded growth clears 15% (my arithmetic). The company does not give the product split. So whether the pillar changes depends on a number that is not disclosed.
- The money goes into steel taps, the HSS core where TOTEM leads, not carbide. That fits the signed model ("the profit anchor is the dull, dominant half"). It also says nothing for the carbide and aerospace transition.
- The parent corollary on file: a group paying about 19% "needs subsidiary cash", so the payout "will not fall" and that "caps how much... FPTL can self-fund" (V9). With net cash near zero after the dividend, ₹60 Cr of capex means debt. Infomerics rated ₹50.54 Cr of facilities in Feb-2026, of which ₹4.34 Cr term loan and ₹20 Cr working capital were proposed (Notion, Section 5). This capex likely needs more than that [INFERENCE].
- The rationale is one line: "economic indicators reflect potential demand". No customer, no order, no product mix.

### Two readings

Bull:
- Utilisation of 80-92% on a moat product, and a board willing to add 28% capacity, says demand is real.
- Overheads held. The margin still cleared 20%.
- CFO converted at 1.03x. Pre-buying through a tungsten squeeze is what Kennametal did too (inventory tracker note).

Bear:
- Materials at 40.5% is the falsifier firing, not drifting. Pass-through is failing against hedged competitors.
- PAT fell YoY. Growth halved from Q1.
- Inventory and raw material rose again. Net cash is gone. The dividend is still funded from investments. Now debt funds capex too.
- FY27E EPS ₹7.75 needs H2 PAT of ₹23.05 Cr, +38% on H2 FY26's ₹16.65 Cr (my arithmetic). Q2's run rate gives about ₹32.8 Cr for FY27. That is the bear case (₹33 Cr).

What separates them: the Q3 FY27 materials ratio (results about early Feb-2027). Below 36% with APT flat says pass-through is catching up. At or above 36% a third time confirms the row's reading that "the carbide segment is structurally uneconomic for TOTEM".

### Price and valuation

₹148 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:36 IST). 52-week range ₹103 to ₹203. Market cap ₹765 Cr. The filing was signed 17:49 IST on 28-Oct, after the close, so this price likely predates the results [INFERENCE]. My arithmetic, not filed:

- Up 8.8% from ₹136 on file (Notion, 22-Oct).
- Against your logged buy-week prints of ₹155.40 (8-Sep) and ₹161.20 (11-Sep), down 4.8% to 8.2%.
- TTM EPS falls from ₹6.59 to ₹6.52 (Q2 below Q2 FY26). P/E 22.7x trailing, 19.1x on the ₹7.75 FY27E.

| EPS basis | EPS | Track 1 at 14.9x | Track 2 at 18.9x |
|---|---|---|---|
| Trailing | ₹6.52 | ₹97 | ₹123 |
| FY27E (operator) | ₹7.75 | ₹115 | ₹146 |

At ₹148 the price is above every cell. The open trailing-vs-forward ruling no longer changes the answer. If the derived raw-material figure triggers the 0.65x multiplier rule, Track 2 falls from 18.9x to about 12.3x (my arithmetic).

### What it does not say

- No raw material split of inventory. It is the number OR-5 needs.
- No reason for the 31.7% tax rate.
- No product split, so the capex economics cannot be tested.
- No pledge data. The Sep-26 SHP encumbrance table is still unread (pledge tracker, next check 4-Nov).
- No earnings call, as always.

Odd but minor: the notes heading says "for the year ended 30th September, 2026" (p.7), and the cash flow title says "for the the year ended" (p.8). Same sloppiness pattern on file. Dividend paid ₹25.25 Cr against ₹25.80 Cr implied by ₹5 on 5.16 Cr shares (my arithmetic). Likely unclaimed or timing. Not a flag.

### Verdict

The Tier 1 materials row fires on the materials leg, with APT last logged as flat. The Tier 1 inventory row fires. The proof gate's first leg passes by under a point. No thesis-broken trigger on the signed model fires outright, but the two rows the page called thesis-deciding both point against it. The capex is a bet on the core moat product, funded by debt, from a company that is still paying out more than it earns. Your entry is unrecorded, so I cannot test a stop or trim. I read this as moving the holding from "starter case, add reserved for Q2" to no add. Whether to hold or trim is your call.

## Aeroflex: real growth, but Q2 profit carries a depreciation catch-up

Aeroflex is in COMPANIES MASTER as WATCHLIST / HOLD-NOT-ADD. It is held as a 4% momentum trade at ₹230 avg cost, chandelier-governed, with no numeric stop on the page. Entry ₹82-103, MoS ₹82. Five filings came in on 28-Oct. Items 13, 14, 15 and 16 are the same 17-page PDF filed four times (identical file hash): board outcome, results, review reports, CFO annexure. Item 12 is the 33-page investor deck. The 28-Oct gate on file (Notion, Filing Review 20-Oct-2026) has four legs: skid units, skid ASP, H1 CFO/PAT, and the 15K capacity date. Every leg can now be tested.

### The headline: ₹8.41 Cr of Q2 profit is accounting, not operations

[FILED] The company switched depreciation from WDV to straight-line (SLM), "effective from 01.04.2026", accounted prospectively as a change in estimate (results note 5, p.7 and p.13; deck p.9). Q1 had already been reported on WDV, so the whole half-year switch landed in Q2. The auditor spells this out in both review reports. Consolidated Q2 depreciation is "₹44.17 lakhs only which is the balancing amount" for the half year after Q1's WDV charge; standalone it is ₹59.17 lakh (review reports, p.4 and p.11 images). The auditor did not modify the conclusion, but chose to add a paragraph on it.

My arithmetic, not filed, using the deck's own restatement (deck p.9-10):
- Reported Q2 depreciation ₹0.45 Cr. The clean SLM charge for Q2 is ₹4.27 Cr. On WDV it would have been ₹8.86 Cr. Q1 was ₹7.84 Cr and Q2 FY26 was ₹6.10 Cr.
- Q2 PAT on three bases: ₹36.56 Cr reported (+157% YoY), ₹34.00 Cr clean SLM (+139%), ₹28.32 Cr on WDV (+99% YoY, +51% QoQ).
- The deck's "+157%" headline uses the catch-up number. Cash profit (PAT plus depreciation) is the like-for-like line. On the deck it grew +82% YoY in Q2 and +92% in H1, and the Q2 cash-profit margin fell 49 bps YoY to 17.82% (deck p.9).

There is also a tax inconsistency inside the disclosure. The filed notes give the PBT effect as ₹841.25 lakh and the PAT effect as ₹823.48 lakh (consolidated, p.7). That implies tax of only about ₹0.18 Cr on ₹8.41 Cr of lower depreciation. The standalone note is stranger: PBT effect ₹769.02 lakh, PAT effect ₹769.46 lakh (p.13). A PAT effect larger than the PBT effect means negative tax on the change. The deck's illustrative WDV column carries an H1 ETR of 29.8% (₹19.97 / ₹67.09 Cr), against 26.7% reported (₹20.15 / ₹75.50 Cr). At the reported ETR, H1 PAT on WDV would be about ₹49.2 Cr, not ₹47.12 Cr. That puts the true after-tax effect near ₹6.2 Cr, not ₹8.23 Cr. This is my arithmetic, not filed. Either the illustrative table is loose or the deferred-tax entry on the change is off. It is a question for the call, not a finding.

Why this matters for the thesis, beyond the headline:
- Capex absorption was the central bear argument on file. In FY26, D&A rose 131% against revenue up 17.5% (Notion, AR review Step 3), and FY27 D&A was expected at ₹35-40 Cr. Under SLM, H1 D&A is ₹8.29 Cr, which runs at roughly ₹17-20 Cr for the year [INFERENCE]. That argument has been neutralised by an accounting choice, not by utilisation.
- The ROCE below 18% tripwire is now harder to fire for accounting reasons. My arithmetic, H1 annualised on closing capital employed of about ₹521 Cr (equity ₹497.42 plus borrowings ₹0.85 plus leases ₹23.14 Cr, deck p.11): about 29% on SLM and about 26% on WDV. Both are well clear of 18%, so the tripwire does not fire either way. But from here every ROCE print is flattered against the history the tripwire was set on.
- The FY26 AR review logged "no useful life changes". One quarter later the method changed, effective back to 1-Apr, after Q1 was published on WDV and after the 29-Jul call said nothing about it (Notion, Q1 reviews). That is not a restatement and not a qualification, so thesis-broken trigger 3 does not fire. It is the closest thing to that trigger on file.

### The operating quarter is genuinely strong

[FILED] Consolidated Q2 revenue was ₹207.36 Cr, up 87.0% YoY and 42.6% QoQ. EBITDA was ₹50.34 Cr at 24.27% (+93 bps YoY), above the ~23% FY27 guide on file. H1 revenue was ₹352.74 Cr, up 80.7% (deck p.9). EBITDA is untouched by the depreciation change, so this part is clean.

Gate test, leg by leg (gate as written on file, 20-Oct):
- **Skid units: pass, or narrow miss, depending on reading.** Q2 was 1,475 skids (deck p.19), up 41.8% QoQ from 1,040. That passes the 1,300 bull line in the 20-Oct note. It sits just below the "volume above ~1,500" bull line written in the 27-Jul Q1 verdict. Please rule on which bar governs.
- **Skid ASP: pass.** ₹3,39,196 per skid, up 8.9% QoQ (deck p.19). It is the first rise after three straight declines, above the ₹3,10,000 bull line, and above the ₹3.0-3.25 lakh FY27 range guided in May. Skid value was ₹50.03 Cr, 24.1% of revenue (my arithmetic). ASP rose while EBITDA margin also rose, which fits management's "design-driven, margin-neutral" explanation [INFERENCE].
- **H1 CFO/PAT: amber, close to red on reported numbers.** CFO was ₹38.16 Cr against PAT of ₹55.35 Cr, which is 0.69x (deck p.12; my arithmetic). Green is above 0.90x and red is below 0.65x. On the WDV-equivalent PAT of ₹47.12 Cr it is 0.81x, still amber. H1 FY26 was 1.27x (₹27.25 / ₹21.40 Cr) and FY26 was 1.19x. Please rule on which PAT is the denominator: the gate was set before the method change.
- **15K capacity date: fails the open question.** The deck now says 15,000 "by December 2026" (deck p.6, p.14). The concall said Oct-Nov, and the 20-Oct note named "a slip past November" as the open question. This is the third slip: Q2 FY27, then Oct-Nov, now December.
- **ROCE below 18%: does not fire on any basis (see above).** A pinned definition is still missing, and SLM now adds a third version.

Two more checks against logged numbers:
- **Ex-skid business: much faster than guided.** Revenue less skids was ₹157.33 Cr in Q2, against ₹112.99 Cr in Q1 and ₹110.87 Cr in Q2 FY26 (no skids then). That is +39% QoQ and +42% YoY, against a 15-20% base-business guide (Notion, Q4 concall). My arithmetic. At the Q2 run-rate the ex-skid business is about ₹629 Cr a year. The Q1 concall put the peak hose-plus-assembly ceiling at ₹650-675 Cr on 20M metres. So the base business is near its own stated ceiling, which explains the hose expansion that follows.
- **FY27 guide is stale.** 35% growth implies about ₹597 Cr. H1 is already ₹353 Cr. At the Q2 run-rate, FY27 lands near ₹767 Cr (+74%) (my arithmetic). The deck gives no new number.

### What the cash and the balance sheet say

[FILED, deck p.11-12, results p.8 and p.15] Between Mar-26 and Sep-26:
- Receivables rose from ₹129.82 Cr to ₹203.83 Cr (+₹74.0 Cr, +57%). Payables rose ₹55.6 Cr and inventory rose ₹28.6 Cr. Working capital absorbed ₹29.22 Cr.
- The deck claims "improved working capital days" (p.6). On Q2 annualised revenue, receivable days are about 90, against 107 for FY26. On H1 annualised revenue they are about 105 (my arithmetic). The claim holds only on the Q2 base.
- Cash plus bank balances fell from ₹69.65 Cr to ₹28.46 Cr, plus a new ₹4.01 Cr investment that is not described. CWIP rose from ₹23.10 Cr to ₹73.48 Cr. Standalone capex was ₹67.15 Cr in H1 (p.15). So H1 free cash flow was about minus ₹29 Cr (my arithmetic, standalone capex used). The Notion base case had FCF turning positive in FY27-28.
- Right-of-use assets rose from ₹7.41 Cr to ₹22.18 Cr and lease liabilities from ₹7.83 Cr to ₹23.14 Cr, so a new lease of about ₹15 Cr. The deck now lists a Chakan, Pune plant among four (p.27), which the record does not mention. That the lease is Chakan is my inference.
- New and small: a ₹1.50 Cr provision for bad debts in the standalone H1 cash flow (p.15). The AR review logged nil ECL against ₹39.22 Cr of overdue exports, so this is the first provision. The filing does not say which receivable it covers. There is also a ₹0.09 Cr share-based payment line (p.15), although the AR review found no ESOP resolutions.
- OCI is zero again (p.6), the fourth period in a row against a 760+ gratuity workforce.

### New capex: ₹202 Cr to double skids, before 15K is even commissioned

[MGMT] The next phase targets 30 Mn metres of hose (from 20) and 30,000 skids (from 15,000) by March 2028, at about ₹202 Cr total capex, for incremental peak revenue of about ₹500-550 Cr (deck p.6, p.14).

Tested against the record:
- The Q4 concall said further expansion would be considered "only after commissioning" of 15K (Notion, Step 5A). 15K is not commissioned and has slipped to December. The sequencing promise has been dropped.
- The Q1 concall capex envelope was ₹48 Cr for skids plus ₹54 Cr for hoses, said to complete in FY27. The new ₹202 Cr is twice that envelope again.
- Funding is not stated. Net cash excluding leases is about ₹32 Cr. H1 CFO runs at about ₹76 Cr a year, at 0.69x conversion (my arithmetic). Over about 17 months, internal accruals can plausibly cover most of it, but only if cash conversion recovers. Otherwise it means debt or equity. The record has the ₹400 Cr US QIP pillar silently dropped. Silence on funding is the item to ask about.
- On concentration, the skid capacity doubles against an anchor that is still unnamed and India-only exclusive. The deck adds one new phrase: "Approved supplier to a leading global provider of digital infrastructure and thermal management solutions, enabling participation in global data center projects" (deck p.21). That could be the international skid customer promised for "this FY" on the Q1 call, or the same principal's global arm. It does not say which. Until it is named, the doubling is concentration-additive, consistent with the A5 framing on file. Strategic Premium stays at +0.5x.
- On exports, the Americas share was 66% in Q2 and 62% in H1 (deck p.8, chart-derived). Watchlist item 6 is red above 65%. The Q2 read crosses it, the H1 read does not. It is export-led, US-heavy base growth into a tariff regime.

### CFO change: answers open question 11, with a timing oddity

[FILED, p.2, p.16-17] Mustafa Kachwala completes "tenure" as CFO at close of business on 27-Nov-2026 and stays as Whole-time Director and KMP. Drashti Bhil is re-designated CFO from 28-Nov-2026 for three years. She is a CA with 10+ years of experience, at Aeroflex since August 2020, already running finance and accounts, and not related to any director.

- This partly closes question 11 on file ("why no CFO / second finance voice"). A dedicated CFO replaces a combined WTD/CFO paid ₹0.14 Cr (Notion, AR review 9D). Her remuneration is not disclosed.
- She is an internal successor with no prior CFO role, so it is a thin step-up. She starts after the 29-Oct call, so Q2 is the third consecutive call with no CFO on it.
- "Completion of tenure" is unusual wording for a CFO post that has no fixed term. The change was approved at the same meeting as the depreciation method change it will now own. That is probably a coincidence [INFERENCE], but worth a single question.

### Hyd-Air amalgamation: simplification that also removes a window

[FILED, p.1] The board in-principle approved merging Hyd-Air (a 100% subsidiary) under Section 233 fast-track, appointed date 1-Apr-2026, subject to approvals. The scheme has not been filed. [FILED, review report p.4] Hyd-Air's Q2: total assets ₹39.31 Cr, revenue ₹8.01 Cr, PAT ₹0.055 Cr. H1 PAT is therefore about minus ₹0.21 Cr (Q1 loss ₹0.27 Cr, Notion).

Tested: the record flagged the named "diminution in value of investment" line at zero while the only investment was loss-making (Notion, F1). Parent exposure is ₹27.80 Cr of investment plus a ₹5.40 Cr loan. On a common-control merger, the investment is cancelled against net assets in standalone books, so a visible impairment charge is no longer possible [INFERENCE]. The merger also ends the separate other-auditor review and the quarterly Hyd-Air figures in the review report. Revenue is flat at about ₹32 Cr annualised, against ₹31.62 Cr in FY26, which fits the "internal-use only" framing on file. Neutral on economics, slightly negative on visibility.

### Two readings

Bull, with evidence:
- The ex-skid business grew 42% YoY.
- Skid volume rose 42% QoQ with ASP up for the first time.
- EBITDA margin of 24.3% is above guide.
- Even on WDV, H1 EPS of about ₹3.56 annualises to about ₹7.1, close to the ₹8.3 FY28 base EPS on file a year early.
- A finance head is now named.
- Capacity doubling suggests visibility management would not build without.

Bear, with evidence:
- The headline PAT is flattered by ₹8.41 Cr of catch-up, with an unexplained tax effect.
- Cash conversion halved from 1.27x to 0.69x.
- Receivables rose ₹74 Cr in six months.
- Net cash fell from about ₹70 Cr to about ₹32 Cr, and H1 FCF was about minus ₹29 Cr.
- ₹202 Cr of unfunded capex doubles skid capacity for one unnamed customer, before the 15K line it was supposed to follow, and 15K has slipped three times.
- Americas exports are above the red line on the Q2 read.

The single observation that separates them is FY27 full-year CFO/PAT at the audited print (May 2027). At or above 0.90x on reported PAT says the receivables build was Q2 timing. Below 0.65x says growth is being bought with working capital while accounting lifts the P&L. An early read comes from the Q3 balance sheet if the company volunteers one (it is not mandated).

### Fair value and price

[PRICE] The latest price is ₹492 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:35 IST). The 52-week range is ₹158-614 and market cap is ₹6,514 Cr. That read is the 28-Oct close, before results were filed at about 17:37 IST, so it does not price this quarter. The Notion CMP on file is ₹505 (20-Oct-2026).

My arithmetic:
- The price is 4.8x the ₹103 top of entry and 6.0x the ₹82 MoS.
- Trailing EPS is about ₹6.81 (FY26 ₹4.28 less H1 FY26 ₹1.65 plus H1 FY27 ₹4.18), so P/E is about 72x. On WDV EPS it is about 79x.
- The Year-3 base FV on file is ₹178 (EPS ₹8.3 x 21.5x). Even the FY28 bull EPS of ₹10.5 at 21.5x gives ₹226.
- The quarter helps the growth-visibility pillar, but it hurts the cash pillar (0.69x against the 0.90x effective multiplier). The depreciation change should be stripped out of any EPS or ROCE credit before Role 1 is re-run.
- Entry does not move on this print, under the rules on file.

The momentum trade is up about 114% on ₹230. Every logged chart level (₹312 and below) is far under the price, and the numeric chandelier stop is still not set. The ruling has been pending since 20-Oct.

No thesis-broken trigger fires. The accounting-change trigger comes closest and does not fire, because this is prospective and the review conclusion is unmodified. On your rules, I read this as operationally stronger but lower in earnings quality: growth evidence improves, cash and accounting evidence weakens. Net, WATCHLIST / HOLD-NOT-ADD holds and entry is unchanged. Any change is your call.

## Satin Creditcare: gate passes on both ROE bases, but the profit jump is not from lending

Satin is in COMPANIES MASTER as WATCHLIST / BUY. Entry Max is ₹311 and MoS is ₹249. The post-AR ladder is Strong Buy below ₹255 and Buy ₹255-325. The halt-add and stop line is ₹220: two closes below it means reduce 50% (Notion, properties; AR Step 13-W). Position Size reads "1.5-2% starter; 3.5-4% ceiling", but no Avg Cost is on file. Whether you hold it is still not confirmed (Notion, 24-Oct entry).

Items 167 and 168 are the same 16-page file (byte-identical). Item 166 is the press release, item 165 is the 36-page deck, and item 169 is a blank rating update.

### What was filed, in numbers

[FILED, results p.12; PR p.2; deck p.15 and p.30]
- Consolidated Q2 PAT ₹175.9 Cr (₹17,590.35 lakh), against ₹122.65 Cr in Q1 and ₹53 Cr in Q2 FY26. H1 PAT ₹298.5 Cr.
- Standalone Q2 PAT ₹171.6 Cr (results p.4).
- Consolidated AUM ₹16,589 Cr, up 31% YoY and 4% QoQ.
- Consolidated ROE, Q2 annualised: reported 22.8%, "adjusted" 27.7%. H1: reported 19.8%, adjusted 22.2% (deck p.15).
- Standalone GNPA 1.94% (₹197 Cr), NNPA 0.17%, PCR 91.46%, CRAR 27.89% (results p.9).
- Limited review by J C Bhalla & Co is unmodified, both standalone and consolidated (p.2-3, p.10-11).

### Master gate, item #5: green on both bases, so the basis ruling no longer blocks Q2

The gate is consolidated ROE annualised for Q2: green at 17% or more, red below 14% (Notion, 21-item checklist).
- Reported basis: 22.8%. Pass.
- Adjusted basis: 27.7%. Pass.
- I checked the reported figure myself: ₹175.9 Cr × 4 = ₹703.6 Cr, against average equity of about ₹3,080 Cr (Sep-26 total equity ₹3,177.8 Cr, results p.13; Jun-26 net worth ₹2,943.62 Cr, Notion Q1 review). That gives about 22.8%. My arithmetic, not filed.

**Correction to the record.** The deck shows Q1 FY27 consolidated ROE as 16.7% *reported* and 20.4% *adjusted* (deck p.15). Notion logged the 16.75% as "struck ex-overlay (non-GAAP)" (Q1 review). The 23-Oct entry then estimated GAAP ROE at about 13.75%. Both were wrong. Q1 cleared the escalation gate on the reported basis. Standalone matches what Notion logged (Q1 reported 15.1% against adjusted 18.5%, deck p.17).

**How the "adjusted" figures are built.** Deck p.15, note 2: adjusted ratios exclude the management overlay "amounting to ₹50 Crores (Q2FY27), ₹36 Crores (Q1FY27), ₹21 Crores (FY26)" [FILED].
- The company adds back the whole overlay balance in each period, not that quarter's charge.
- The overlay grew from ₹36 Cr to ₹50 Cr, so the visible Q2 increment is ₹14 Cr. My arithmetic.
- So the 27.7% adds back about 3.6 times the visible Q2 build. Adjusted credit cost of 0.99% is the same: (₹83.2 Cr Q2 impairment less ₹50 Cr) × 4 on about ₹13,460 Cr average AUM.
- The filing does not say whether the ₹36 Cr was released and ₹50 Cr charged fresh. If it was not, the adjusted series overstates run-rate profitability every quarter. [INFERENCE]
- **Recommendation: rule the gate onto the reported basis.** The time stop then reads cleanly: "Q3 FY27 ROE not above 16% sustained = exit" (Notion, Verdict Card).

### Where the extra profit came from

Consolidated PBT rose ₹74 Cr QoQ, from ₹161 Cr to ₹235 Cr (deck p.30). Split into its lines (my arithmetic, from deck p.30):
- Interest income +₹40 Cr, finance cost +₹26 Cr, opex +₹23 Cr. Net: interest spread less opex fell ₹9 Cr, from ₹133 Cr to ₹124 Cr.
- DA (direct assignment) income +₹40 Cr, from ₹94 Cr to ₹134 Cr.
- Other income +₹27 Cr, from ₹40 Cr to ₹67 Cr.
- Credit cost −₹16 Cr, from ₹106 Cr to ₹90 Cr, even with the overlay rising.

So "highest ever profitability" (PR p.2) came from three things: upfront DA gains, other income, and lower provisions. The core lending spread after costs went down.

The filed P&L tells the same story:
- Net gain on fair value changes: +₹26.5 Cr in Q2 against −₹56.7 Cr in Q1.
- The FX line inside finance cost was a ₹62.4 Cr gain in Q1 and a ₹4.1 Cr gain in Q2 (results p.12).
- Netted, these treasury and hedge lines gave about ₹31 Cr in Q2 against about ₹6 Cr in Q1. My arithmetic.

**DA detail** [FILED, standalone note 7]:
- ₹1,132.5 Cr of loans assigned in Q2, 2.61 lakh loans.
- ₹132.6 Cr of interest spread booked to P&L in the quarter.
- Weighted residual maturity 18.7 months; 10% retained.
- That spread is income from roughly 18 months of future interest, booked now. [INFERENCE]
- DA income was about 57% of Q2 PBT, close to Q1's 58%. My arithmetic. The share is steady, but the QoQ increase is mostly DA.

This is not a red flag. DA is an established funding channel and the share is inside your norm. It does mean Q2 is not a clean run-rate.
