**The gate, leg by leg** (01-Aug scorecard, re-set to H1)

- EBITDA at least 10%: **pass**. 12.9% in Q2, 11.2% for H1.
- Product Solutions at least ₹100 Cr: **fail**. ₹69.37 Cr. The forward-trigger version (₹1,000M for two consecutive quarters, Section 6 #3) also fails.
- Consolidated CFO positive: **fail on the sign, by ₹23 lakh**. The 07-Jul lines were bull "any positive", bear "worse than minus ₹15 Cr". H1 sits between them. It is far better than the "large consumption of cash" guided on 04-Aug (t37). But it was carried by ₹53.5 Cr of payables and provisions while inventory rose ₹59 Cr (my arithmetic, not filed). Standalone CFO was plus ₹47.07 Cr (p.18). So the subsidiaries and consolidation adjustments absorbed about ₹47 Cr (my arithmetic, rough, includes intra-group flows).
- Working-capital strategy disclosed: **fail**. The release describes supply measures: more suppliers, BOM changes, earlier customer planning (image p.22). There is no inventory target, no cash-conversion target and no word on the borrowing.
- Top-5 at or below 56%: **pass**, at 49%. Top-3 is still not given.

The gate needs all five. It is **not cleared** for a second time. Please rule whether minus ₹0.23 Cr counts as a fail on the sign or as breakeven. My read: a fail as written, but the trigger (c) direction is better than the 04-Aug guidance implied.

**Two passes are weaker than they look.**
- Top-5 fell from 56% to 49% in one quarter while Product Solutions fell 42%. Borqs revenue is the concentrated part of the book, so concentration fell mostly because hardware shrank [INFERENCE].
- The margin pass is real. Services drove it: segment result up ₹13.71 Cr QoQ while Product Solutions fell ₹2.22 Cr (p.10). Tax did not flatter it: ETR rose from 20.5% to 27.8%.

**The treasury is shrinking and the company now borrows.** Net cash and investments, on the same basis as the ₹374 Cr on file, are ₹335.4 Cr at Sep-26: cash ₹94.46 Cr + other bank ₹39.66 Cr + investments ₹282.02 Cr, less borrowings ₹80.76 Cr (my arithmetic, not filed; the Mar-26 figures on the same basis give ₹373.9 Cr, which matches Notion). That is ₹38.5 Cr down in six months. In H1 the group paid out ₹19.74 Cr in dividend, ₹18.68 Cr in contingent consideration, ₹10.06 Cr in capex and ₹6.91 Cr in lease payments, against CFO of zero. The ₹80.76 Cr borrowing funded the gap. It is three times the ₹26.93 Cr MF-backed facility on file (AR flag 10).

**Silicon has gone quiet.** The AR's only clean positive was Sasken Silicon PAT of about ₹11.80 Cr, back-solved from NCI of ₹4.72 Cr. H1 FY27 NCI is minus ₹0.27 Cr (p.7). If the NCI is all the 40% Silicon minority, the Silicon chain lost about ₹0.66 Cr in H1 (my arithmetic, an assumption). That fits the 04-Aug remark that Silicon productization is part of the margin drag. Trigger (d), Silicon below ₹50 Cr, is still not assessable: revenue is again not disclosed.

**Two readings of Product Solutions**
- Bull: the inventory is memory bought ahead of tight supply; the CFO said so on 04-Aug. Shipments resume in H2. The hyperscaler mandate widened to turnkey development and lifecycle management, and a new device (Juniper Alta) shipped [MGMT, image p.22]. Management now says Product Solutions "is best assessed annually" (image p.24).
- Bear: revenue fell 42% while inventory rose ₹29.1 Cr in the quarter and segment assets rose 21%. At ₹69 Cr a quarter, Borqs runs far below the roughly ₹120 Cr quarterly breakeven given on 04-Aug. Borrowed money is funding the stock. The "assess annually" framing arrived in the quarter the segment collapsed. H1 Product Solutions is ₹189.05 Cr. To reach the ₹400 Cr FY27 line that the AR review tied to goodwill impairment risk (Note 6), H2 needs ₹210.95 Cr, about ₹105 Cr a quarter (my arithmetic).
- What separates them: the Q3 deck, late Jan-2027. Q3 has no cash flow statement, but the deck carries a quarterly balance sheet (p.54). Bull if Product Solutions is at least ₹100 Cr and inventory falls below ₹65 Cr (the Jun level). Bear if it is below ₹100 Cr with inventory still at ₹94 Cr or more. Whether the ₹80.76 Cr is repaid is the cross-check.

**Fair value consequence.** The P&L is running ahead of the projections. TTM owners' EPS is ₹55.44 (FY26 ₹35.61 less H1 FY26 ₹12.68 plus H1 FY27 ₹32.51; my arithmetic). That is above the FY27 step of even the bull path (₹40 x 1.30 = ₹52) and 80% of the base FY29 EPS of about ₹69 (AR Step 13B). The cash leg is not. The 01-Aug note said only "a positive, sustained H1 CFO" lifts the cash multiplier off 0.65x. That did not happen, so the multiplier stays frozen and the earnings beat does not move the committed fair value of ₹784. The note's own refresh trigger has now fired: a committed Stage 11 run is due on this print.

**What it does not say**
- Who received the ₹18.68 Cr contingent consideration, and for which deal. The AR put the Borqs consideration at ₹256.02 Cr. Whether this sits inside it is not stated.
- Which subsidiary borrowed, from whom, at what rate, and against what security.
- No contingent-liabilities note again, so the ₹295.78 Cr disputed tax and the ₹25.35 Cr GST orders of 22-Sep (Notion, 23-Sep) are not mentioned.
- The other-auditor subsidiary is still unnamed. Its Q2 alone was about ₹54.40 Cr revenue and ₹0.79 Cr PAT (H1 less Q1 on file; my arithmetic).
- Standalone other expenses jumped to ₹33.00 Cr from ₹22.16 Cr in Q1 (p.16), while consolidated other expenses fell (p.7). That looks like intra-group charges [INFERENCE]. If so, the standalone-versus-consolidated PAT gap (₹28.93 Cr against ₹25.59 Cr this quarter) no longer measures subsidiary drag cleanly.

**Inconsistencies**
- The deck's "About Sasken" slide still says "Cash Positive, Debt Free" (p.62). The balance sheet four slides earlier shows ₹80.76 Cr of borrowings. That is a factual error in a filed document.
- The release reports "Cash & Cash equivalents" of ₹398.8 Cr (image p.22). The balance sheet line of that name is ₹94.46 Cr (p.8). The ₹398.8 Cr is a gross treasury figure that ignores the debt.
- The 22-Oct filing said payment "on or before 27-Nov". Today's says 26-Nov. Trivial.

**The dividend ruling, pending since 23-Oct.** Its condition is now met literally: H1 CFO is negative and the interim is ₹12. The cost is about ₹18.2 Cr on 1.519 Cr shares (my arithmetic). By the 22-Oct threshold (better than minus ₹15 Cr) it is fine. But the group borrowed ₹80.76 Cr in the same half. Please rule: capital-allocation flag, or neutral.

**The call is today, 29-Oct at 15:00.** The written analyst letter again gives no Silicon revenue (Q11) and no working-capital strategy (Q12). The repeated-evasion clock runs on the call, not the letter. The ruling on whether the 4-flag rule still applies after Q1 is still open (26-Oct). The borrowing and the contingent payment should now be the first two questions.

**Price:** ₹1,848 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:39 IST). 52-week range ₹991-2,785, market cap ₹2,807 Cr. Results were filed at about 20:56 IST on 28-Oct, after the close, so this price does not yet reflect them. My arithmetic, not filed:
- Up 0.8% from ₹1,834 on file (screener.in, 26-Oct).
- 2.36x the ₹784 AVOID line.
- 4.4% above the bull SOTP of about ₹1,770, and 57% above the probability-weighted SOTP of about ₹1,180 (Notion, 01-Aug).
- 33.3x TTM owners' EPS of ₹55.44.

**Verdict in your frame.** No thesis-broken trigger fires. (a) EBITDA is 12.9%. (b) Top-5 is 49%. (c) H1 CFO is minus ₹0.23 Cr, but (c) is a full-year test. (d) and Top-3 are still not assessable. (e) Goodwill is ₹202.72 Cr, up from ₹199.97 Cr on FX only. The P&L leg moved clearly toward the bull side. The cash leg did not move, and the balance sheet got weaker: net treasury fell ₹38.5 Cr and the debt-free status ended. Price is in the AVOID band. I read this as AVOID unchanged, with the gate re-set to the FY27 annual cash flow (May-2027) and the Q3 deck as the interim check. That is your call.

## Aarti Pharma: profit doubles, cash does not, CFO quit in July

Aarti PharmaLabs is in COMPANIES MASTER at WATCHLIST, on an April-2026 stub (EM 47). No Gate 0, no Entry Price Max, no MoS, no position on file (Notion, properties and 23-Oct Key Notes). No tracker rows are linked.

Items 5, 6, 7, 8 and 9 are the same 16-page document. Items 6, 7, 8 and 9 have identical text. Item 5 is the "clear version" re-upload, filed 57 minutes later (signature 21:49 against 20:52, p.2). Its numbers match item 7 except where its own OCR misreads digits; I checked the two conflicts that matter (consolidated March other financial assets and the H1 FY26 payables line) against the totals, and item 7 is right. Page 16 of every copy is the same image: Piyush Lakhani's handwritten-accepted resignation letter. Item 10 is a separate 1-page ESOP grant. All read in full.

### What it says, in numbers [FILED]

Consolidated, ₹ Cr (Aarti_Pharma_..._7496bf, p.13; converted from ₹ lakh):

| Line | Q2 FY27 | Q1 FY27 | Q2 FY26 (restated) | H1 FY27 | H1 FY26 (restated) | FY26 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Revenue from operations | 610.5 | 535.8 | 418.3 | 1,146.2 | 804.5 | 1,819.4 |
| Share of JV profit | 6.9 | 7.4 | (0.6) | 14.3 | (2.4) | 8.0 |
| Exceptional item | (2.0) | 0 | 0 | (2.0) | 0 | (2.8) |
| PAT | 71.5 | 76.1 | 20.5 | 147.6 | 66.6 | 174.7 |
| EPS (₹) | 7.88 | 8.40 | 2.26 | 16.28 | 7.34 | 19.27 |

- Balance sheet at 30-Sep (p.14): inventories ₹794.2 Cr (₹640.3 Cr in March), trade receivables ₹685.0 Cr (₹583.3 Cr), current borrowings ₹676.6 Cr (₹499.8 Cr), non-current borrowings ₹159.8 Cr (₹185.8 Cr). Cash ₹4.2 Cr plus other bank balances ₹19.1 Cr. Equity ₹2,254.8 Cr.
- H1 cash flow (p.15): operating profit before working capital ₹271.4 Cr; working capital absorbed ₹213.6 Cr; CFO ₹30.0 Cr (H1 FY26 ₹96.5 Cr). Capex ₹142.8 Cr. Net borrowing +₹137.8 Cr.
- Note 3, stamp duty (p.13): the Gandhinagar stamp authority's order of 29-Aug-2026 sets ₹25.0 Cr duty plus ₹34.5 Cr penalty on the 2022 NCLT demerger order from Aarti Industries. The company applied for adjudication in Nov-2022. It has filed a writ in the Gujarat High Court; "no interim order has been passed". It provides ₹2.04 Cr as exceptional and shows ₹57.46 Cr as contingent.
- Note 5 (p.13): Q2 and H1 FY26 are restated for a fair value loss of ₹9.93 Cr and ₹14.57 Cr on a target redemption forward contract (TARF) entered in FY25. Restated Q2 FY26 PAT is ₹7.43 Cr lower, H1 ₹10.90 Cr lower.
- Auditor Gokhale & Sathe: unmodified, with Emphasis of Matter on both Note 3 and Note 5 (p.6, p.11).
- CFO: Piyush Lakhani relieved at close of business 30-Oct-2026 "to pursue an alternate career opportunity outside the organization". Vibha Thakkar CFO from 31-Oct: CA, 18+ years, most recently AVP Finance & Accounts at NOCIL, earlier VIP Industries, Greaves Cotton, Sandoz, KPMG (p.1, p.3).
- Board met 2:00 pm to 7:10 pm (p.2).

### The arithmetic against the record (my arithmetic, not filed)

Growth. Q2 revenue +45.9% YoY and +13.9% QoQ. H1 +42.5%. H1 PAT ₹147.6 Cr is already 84% of FY26's ₹174.7 Cr. Against the restated base, Q2 PAT is +249%. Against the Q2 FY26 PAT as first reported (₹20.5 + ₹7.4 = ₹27.9 Cr), it is +156%. Both are large; the restatement makes the headline look bigger. The JV (Ganesh Polychem, equity method from Apr-25 per Notion) swung from a ₹2.4 Cr loss to a ₹14.3 Cr profit in H1. That is 10% of H1 PAT.

Margin. EBITDA ex other income: Q2 ₹135.2 Cr (22.1%), Q1 ₹136.1 Cr (25.4%), Q2 FY26 ₹64.4 Cr (15.4%, or 17.9% before the restated FX loss). Q2 EBITDA was flat on Q1 while revenue grew ₹75 Cr. Material cost (consumed plus traded plus stock change) went from 44.2% of revenue in Q1 to 49.0% in Q2. That is the whole margin loss. The filing gives no reason.

Cash. CFO/PAT in H1 is 0.20 (₹30.0 / ₹147.6 Cr). Inventory rose ₹153.9 Cr and receivables ₹101.6 Cr in six months. Net debt went from about ₹676 Cr in March to about ₹813 Cr at 30-Sep. Net debt / equity 0.36x. The P&L credit for inventory build (H1 change in inventories ₹97.1 Cr) is profit not yet in cash. Receivable days on annualised H1 revenue are about 109, against 117 at March, so collection is not worse; the cash went into stock and capex.

Valuation on the record. The 22-Sep Key Notes priced the stock at "~41x normalized EPS ~Rs22 [INFERENCE]". Trailing EPS is now ₹28.21 (FY26 ₹19.27 less H1 FY26 ₹7.34 plus H1 FY27 ₹16.28). At ₹882 that is 31.3x trailing and 27.1x on H1 annualised. The ₹22 figure on file is stale. "15-18% guided growth" on file is running at nearly three times that in H1.

Stamp duty. ₹57.46 Cr contingent is 2.5% of equity and 39% of H1 PAT. With no stay, the demand is enforceable while the writ runs [INFERENCE on the legal effect]. The provision of ₹2.04 Cr is the company's view of the duty it accepts. If the full ₹59.5 Cr crystallised, it would be about ₹6.6 a share before tax effects.

### What it touches on file

- The 22-Sep "FY25 restatement flag worth checking against the FY26 AR accounting-policy note" (Notion, 22-Sep Key Notes). Closed in part. The auditor now carries an Emphasis of Matter on the restated FY26 interim periods for a TARF loss. A TARF is a leveraged exotic FX structure, not a plain hedge [INFERENCE on the instrument]. The restatement means the loss was not booked in the interim periods when first reported. That is a treasury and close-process question.
- The 23-Oct entry logged Lakhani as a named CFO speaker on the 29-Oct call, "consistent with the 01-Oct-26 handover" (Notion, 23-Oct Key Notes). The image page changes that reading. The resignation letter is dated "8/7/2026" and marked "Accepted" by a Gogri signature the same day, with "Reliving Date 31.10.2026" handwritten (p.16, image). In Indian date format that is 8-Jul-2026. If read month-first it is 7-Aug. Either way, the CFO resigned 82 to 112 days before this disclosure.
  - He resigned before the 22-Sep AGM, before the 1-Oct MD handover, and before the 22-Oct call intimation that named him CFO.
  - Whether the gap is compliant depends on which LODR clause the company relies on. The KMP-resignation clause runs from the date the resignation takes effect, which is 30-Oct [INFERENCE on the rule]. On that reading it is on time. On the 24-hour change-in-KMP reading it is not. I am not calling it a breach. I am noting that the market was not told for about three and a half months.
- The handover pattern. The 22-Sep note logged the same 1-Oct handover at Aarti Drugs. Here a new MD takes over on 1-Oct and the CFO who resigned in July leaves four weeks later. The successor's last role was AVP, one level below CFO, at a company outside the group.
- Promoter watch items (Rashesh Gogri's holding, 30-Sep shareholding pattern): not in these filings. Still open.
- Atali 80%, Tarapur 9,600 TPA vs 9,000 MTPA, Atali Block-2 groundbreaking: the results are silent. One segment only (Note 6). The revenue jump fits Tarapur and Atali coming on line [INFERENCE].

### Gates

No gate, entry line or tripwire is on file. Nothing to test leg by leg. The stub basis "alpha window too far" was already marked stale on 22-Sep. This print is the input the missing Gate 0 / Role 1 pass would need.

### Two readings

Bull:
- New capacity is reaching revenue: +46% YoY and +14% QoQ, with H1 PAT 84% of last year.
- The JV turned from loss to ₹14 Cr profit.
- The restatement is now disclosed, audited and behind the company.
- The new CFO brings Big 4 and specialty-chemicals experience (NOCIL).

Bear:
- Profit is not turning into cash. CFO/PAT 0.20, inventory and receivables up ₹256 Cr, short-term debt up ₹177 Cr.
- Q2 margin fell 330 bps QoQ on material cost, unexplained.
- The CFO resigned in July and it was disclosed on results day. In between came the AGM, the MD change and a call intimation naming him CFO.
- ₹57.5 Cr stamp demand with no stay.
- Treasury ran a TARF big enough to force a restatement.

The one observation that separates them: Q3 FY27 results (by mid-Feb 2027). EBITDA margin ex other income back to 24% or more, and total borrowings at or below the ₹836 Cr of 30-Sep. Both met says the growth is self-funding. Margin near 22% with borrowings rising again says H1 profit was bought with working capital.

### What it does not say

- Why material cost rose 4.8 points of revenue in one quarter.
- When the stamp order was disclosed. It is dated 29-Aug. The 22-Sep and 23-Oct Notion entries do not mention it. My filing windows start 9-Oct, so I cannot see an Aug or Sep disclosure. NOT FOUND, not proven absent. A ₹34.5 Cr penalty order would usually need its own Reg 30 filing [INFERENCE on the rule].
- Whether Aarti Industries, the demerging company, shares the stamp duty under the scheme.
- Whether the TARF is closed, its notional, and its remaining fair value.
- Why the resignation was held from July to October.

### Internal inconsistencies

- Board outcome: relieved "close of business hours on October 30, 2026" (p.1). Handwritten on the letter: 31.10.2026 (p.16). 31-Oct is a Saturday. Same effect, minor.
- Screener's summary for item 9 says "Q2/H1 FY26". The filing is FY27. Screener error, not the company's.
- The first upload was unreadable enough to need a "clear version" within the hour. Cosmetic.

### Briefly: the ESOP grant (item 10)

850 performance options at ₹5 (face value) under PSOP 2023 (Intimation_e809f2, p.1). At ₹882 that is about ₹7.5 lakh of value. Immaterial. H1 ESOP cost is ₹1.6 Cr (p.15).

### Cross-links

- Aarti Drugs: the same 1-Oct handover date is logged there (Notion, 22-Sep). Watch whether its CFO or KMP changes too.
- Aarti Industries: the demerging company in the stamp-duty order.

### Price

₹882 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:35 IST). 52-week ₹584 to ₹1,002. Market cap ₹7,998 Cr. This is the 28-Oct close, before the results filed at 20:52 [INFERENCE from timings]. On file ₹829 (Notion, 23-Oct-2026): up 6.4%. No entry, MoS, stop or trim line exists to test against. 31.3x trailing EPS.

No thesis-broken trigger exists to fire. The P&L is far ahead of the stub's assumptions; the cash and the disclosure record are behind. I read this as a reason to run the full pass the file says is missing, with cash conversion and the CFO timeline as the first two questions. WATCHLIST is your call.

## Paisalo Digital: promoter shares now secure a stranger's loan; the call offers no cost-to-income relief

Paisalo is in COMPANIES MASTER. Decision Status is WATCHLIST. An AVOID recommendation has waited for your ratification since 6-Aug. On 23-Oct it got a filed basis: in Q2 both gate legs failed, with cost-to-income at 41.0% and fees at ₹10.12 Cr (Notion, 23-Oct filing review). Nothing is held. Three items came in on 28-Oct. Two of them are one event.

**Items 120 and 121 are the same pledge, not a trade.** Screener lists item 120 as a "Trade of 10,363,523 equity worth Rs 7959.19 lacs by promoter group". The Reg 31 filing has the same share count and the same value, ₹79.59 Cr (Paisalo_..._SEB_70766a.txt, p.3, p.5). It is a pledge. No promoter holding moved: the group still holds 42,51,17,304 shares, the same figure as on file (p.2-3; Notion, 01-Oct review). Trigger (e), promoter selling above 1% in a quarter, does not fire.

### The pledge [FILED]

- Equilibrated Venture Cflow (EVC) pledged 1,03,63,523 shares, 1.10% of capital, on 26-Oct-2026 to **Jio Credit Limited** (p.3).
- The stated reason: "Collateral for loans taken by the Third Party" (p.3, p.5). The purpose lines for "personal use by promoters" and "benefit of listed company" are both blank (p.5).
- The amount is ₹25 Cr against shares valued at ₹79.59 Cr. The filing gives the ratio as 3.18 (p.5). That works out to ₹76.80 a share (my arithmetic, not filed).
- EVC's encumbered shares went from 2,97,95,479 to 4,01,59,002. That is 4.27% of capital and 20.70% of EVC's own block (p.3-4).

**What is new is the borrower, not the lender.** Every pledge on record so far secured a promoter's own borrowing: warrant-conversion loans, margin trading, or loans against security (Notion, 1-Jul, 3-Jul, 4-Sep, 30-Sep notes). This one secures a loan to someone else, and the filing does not name them. If that borrower defaults, Jio Credit can sell promoter shares. The promoters' own credit would not be the cause.

Group pledge, my arithmetic, not filed, from the table on p.2-3:

- Before this event: 1,11,29,933 + 1,07,59,774 + 94,84,901 + 94,93,926 + 2,97,95,479 = 7,06,64,013 shares, 7.52% of 94.01 Cr shares.
- After: 8,10,27,536 shares. That is **8.62% of capital** and 19.06% of promoter holding.
- On file (14-Oct): 7,22,23,004 shares, 7.68% (Notion, Key Notes 14-Oct).

Gate tests, leg by leg:

- Trigger (c), pledge above 15% of capital: pass, at 8.62%.
- Devil's advocate signal #7 and the reflexivity tracker's "confirmed" leg, pledge above 9%: **pass, but only by 0.38 points.** About 35.8 lakh more shares, roughly ₹28 Cr at ₹79, would cross it (my arithmetic). This is the closest the group has come to that line on our figures. The 1-Jul note logged about 10.23%, but that was on the old share base and before the summed-table method.
- 1-Jul tripwire, any tranche below 1.5x cover or any invocation: pass. The cover is 3.18x, the highest on the trail. The cover falls to 1.5x only near ₹36 a share (my arithmetic).
- 4-Sep lender-quality condition, pledges moving to unregulated private companies: **depends on reading.**
  - Reading A: Jio Credit is a regulated NBFC. That is my inference; the YES/NO box on p.5 is not readable in the text layer. On this reading the condition does not fire, and the trail moves back toward regulated lenders.
  - Reading B: the condition was written about counterparty risk. A third-party borrower nobody has named is a counterparty risk the condition did not foresee.
  - Please rule whether third-party collateral pledges belong under that condition or need a tripwire of their own.

What the filing does not say, but should:

- Who the third party is, and whether it is related to the promoters. The form's "names of all other entities in the agreement" line reads "N.A." (p.5). That leaves out the borrower, who is plainly a party to the arrangement.
- The tenor of the loan, and what EVC gets in return for pledging its shares.

Inconsistencies inside the filing:

- **About 15.6 lakh pledged shares are missing without a filing.** On file (14-Oct) against this filing's pre-event column: Sunil Agarwal is down 2,26,067, Santanu Agarwal 2,96,226, Pro Fitcch 3,11,101, Pri Caf 3,71,076 and EVC 3,54,521. That is 15,58,991 in all (my arithmetic). Holdings did not change, so these are releases, not invocations. No release filing for them is on the record. The odd-lot amounts point to broker margin adjustments [INFERENCE]. The tracker logs a 27.96 lakh Santanu release on 9-Oct [SECONDARY], which does not match his 2.96 lakh change.
- **Encumbrance number 48 is used twice.** The 1-Oct filing used "Encumbrance 48" for EVC's 40 lakh release from STCI, dated 01.10.2026 (Notion, 01-Oct review). This schedule gives number 48 to the 26-Oct Jio pledge, and the STCI release no longer appears (p.5). The schedule still leaves out the 3-Sep Imperial Solutions and Mahacol Trexim pledges. The annexure's signature date is "30.09.2026" (p.5), four weeks before the event it reports.
- The p.4 figure "20.70% of promoter shareholding" is EVC's own block. For the whole group the figure is 19.06% (my arithmetic).

This is the third disclosure-quality item in a month, after the 14-Oct count corrections and the 23-Oct BSE fine. The schedule cannot be relied on to the share.

### Item 122: the Q2 call transcript (22-Oct call, filed 28-Oct) [MGMT]

The CFO was present this time; he was absent in Q1. The call was hosted by Elara. Six questions were asked. Nobody asked for the cost-to-income figure, the fee line, the ₹0.24 Cr provision write-back, the pledges, the FCCB or the ₹155.7 Cr rise in investments.

**Trigger (d), cost-to-income, and gate (i).** No ratio was given. Asked directly when the cost-to-income ratio would improve, the DMD replied that the AI and human frameworks are running in parallel, "which is causing that blip in the operating leverage". He placed the turn at "the end of that third fiscal year" of the three-year plan (p.10). He also said the 11% headcount cut will "normalize" in Q3-Q4 (p.8). On the filed basis, trigger (d) is at strike one (Q2 41.0%), and Q3 FY27 decides (Notion, 23-Oct). Management's own words point to no cost relief in Q3. A second filed quarter above 35% would fire (d) whichever way you rule on the spoken versus filed basis.

**Fees, gate (ii) and trigger (b).** Co-lending is "status quo", waiting on the banks' systems (p.8). Nothing here helps the ₹12 Cr gate or the 5%-of-income line (Q2 was 3.65%, Notion 23-Oct).

**The write-back is explained by nobody.** The CFO says "Credit cost declined to 0.4% in quarter 2" and that H1 provisions were ₹15.5 Cr (p.7). Filed Q1 was ₹15.73 Cr and Q2 was a ₹0.24 Cr write-back (Notion, 23-Oct). Q1 plus Q2 = ₹15.49 Cr, so the 0.4% is an H1 figure given as Q2 (my arithmetic). That confirms the inference on file about the ROE tree. Q2 on its own was negative credit cost.

**Asset quality has a new risk on the way in.** The CBO names Maharashtra as "the most impacted state" from the monsoon. Some districts there have been declared drought-affected, and the company is moderating rural and B2B disbursements (p.5). Maharashtra is 20.1% of AUM (Notion, deck p.18). Agri and allied is about 15% of the book (p.3). Agri equipment repays half-yearly, so "it is still early to draw definitive conclusions" (p.5). Trigger (a), GNPA above 1.5%, is far off at 0.66%. But Q2's write-back and Q3's drought exposure meet in the same credit-cost line. The Q3 credit cost is the number to read.

**NIM, monitorable #6.** Management says NIM "will be range bound within the expectation... about 6% on an annualized basis. We are currently at 5.7%" (p.7-8). That 5.7% is the deck "NIM", which is yield less cost of borrowing. The ruling on file asks which NIM governs (Notion, 23-Oct). On the deck basis, management's own full-year guide sits at the 6.0% red line and below the 6.5-7.0% green band. This makes the definition ruling more pressing.

**Funding.** Management assumes a 25-30 bps rise in cost of funds after a 25 bps repo increase. Cost of funds is now 10.0% against a full-year guide of 10.5% (p.7, p.12). Monitorable #5 (green at 10.5% or less) is guided to finish on the line. The ₹124.47 Cr unsecured NCD at 12.00% (Notion, 23-Oct) is called "the unsecured 10-year paper" (p.11). That is new to the record: the 12% coupon is fixed for ten years, unless there is a call option, and none is stated.

**Yield.** The explanation is the same as in the deck: disbursements and funding came late in the quarter, and "nothing that we are doing in terms of yield reduction" (p.9). The CFO's own number undercuts it. Q2 disbursement was ₹1,567 Cr, up 42% YoY (p.6), but down 9% QoQ (Notion, 23-Oct). Q1 was bigger. The DMD also mentions "tariff practice and the drought affected areas" holding back growth (p.8). That is a second cause, offered in passing.

**Claims that do not match the record:**

- "We also doubled our ROE and ROA in the last 2 fiscal years from about 1.5% and 7% to now 13.3% and 3.5%" (p.10). The record shows ROE at 13.5% in FY24, 13.1% in FY25 and 13.2% in FY26 (Notion, 3.3 value-trap test). Over those two years ROE has been flat, not doubled.
- The long-term ROE aspiration is now "15% to 18%" (p.10). The 9-Oct Bharat Connect compendium had FY30 ROE at 20% [MGMT via broker] (Notion, 09-Oct). That is a cut, unless "long-term" and "FY30" mean different things.
- Bots: "25 live AI bots" (p.4); then Q1 had "basically 0 bots" and "0 text bots, then we had 7 and now we are at 15" (p.10). The Q1 call record says bots went from 7 to 18 (Notion, 6-Aug). These three counts cannot all be true.

### Two readings

- Bull:
  - The pledge cover is 3.18x and the lender is regulated.
  - Total income rose 24% and AUM 33%, while GNPA is 0.66% and collection efficiency 98.3%.
  - Cost of funds is 10.0%, inside the 10.5% guide, and the CFO was on the call.
  - The app launch is dated for the end of Q3.
- Bear:
  - Promoter shares now back an unnamed third party's debt.
  - The group pledge is 0.38 points under the 9% line, and 15.6 lakh shares moved without a filing on the record.
  - Management places cost-to-income relief two to three years out.
  - Co-lending is idle, and NIM is guided to the red line.
  - The provision write-back is unexplained, while drought hits a state that is a fifth of the book.
- What separates them:
  - Filed Q3 FY27 cost-to-income, with results around late January 2027. At 35% or below, the bull case survives. Above 35%, trigger (d) fires on filed numbers.
  - On the pledge side: group pledge above 9% of capital, or any filing naming the third-party borrower as promoter-linked, before the Q3 print, confirms the bear case.

### Fair value and price

No pillar moves. The transcript confirms the 23-Oct read that Pillar 3 (+0.15x growth visibility) looks generous. The Step 7 downside case on file (FV ₹60, entry ₹31, MoS ₹25) still describes the quarter (Notion, Q4 FY26 Step 7).

₹79.0 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:38 IST), 52-week ₹30.0-94.9. Screener's market cap of ₹7,219 Cr still uses about 91.4 Cr shares. On 94.01 Cr shares it is about ₹7,427 Cr (my arithmetic). Against the lines on file:

- 2.14x the ₹37 entry and 2.72x the ₹29 MoS.
- 10% above the post-conversion Year 3 base of ₹71.9.
- 8% above the ₹73 reflexivity line.
- About 3.6x post-conversion book of ₹21.85.
- Down from ₹79.3 on file (Notion, 23-Oct).

No new thesis-broken trigger fires. Trigger (d) is at strike one on filed numbers, and management has now guided against a Q3 recovery. The pledge moves toward the 9% line through a borrower the record cannot see. On your rules, AVOID stands, now on a filed basis since 23-Oct. Ratification, and the new third-party pledge question, are your call.

## TAAL Tech: proof-gate date set, price already below entry zone

TAAL Tech is in COMPANIES MASTER. Decision Status is blank ("ruling pending, operator-only", Notion Key Notes 22-Sep). Position NOT HELD. The Entry Price Max and MoS properties are empty. The levels on file are in the Key Notes: post-split entry zone ₹880-900 for a 25% one-year return on base at 25-25.6x, MoS ₹700, and ₹630 on the three-year Track 2 hurdle (Notion, Key Notes 22-Sep; FY27 valuation table). I read the full page and seven of the 15 linked tracker rows: all five Tier 1 rows, USD/INR, and post-split SHP. The other eight are Tier 2-3 rows with no check date in this window. This is the first TAAL filing in any run INDEX on disk since 09-Oct.

**What it says [FILED]** (TAAL_Tech_Board_Meeting_Intimation_for_The_Board_M_dce92d.txt, p.1):
- The board meets Tuesday 3-Nov-2026 to approve standalone and consolidated results for Q2 and H1 FY27.
- The same meeting will consider a "First Interim Dividend for the Financial Year 2026-27". The record date is 13-Nov-2026 and payment is due on or before 2-Dec-2026.
- The trading window has been closed since 1-Oct. It reopens 48 hours after the results.
- There is no earnings call, no investor presentation and no dividend amount.

### The proof gate now has a date

The H1 FY27 proof gate on file (Notion, Mental Model; tracker Tier 1 rows) has these legs:

- Revenue leg: the quarter must be above ₹57-60 Cr AND above about $6.3m at the quarter-average reference rate. The tracker logs the Q2 average at about 95.6 per USD (tracker USD/INR row, SECONDARY). $6.3m × 95.6 = about ₹60.2 Cr. My arithmetic, not filed. So the dollar leg binds, and the real line is about ₹60 Cr, not ₹57 Cr. Q1 was ₹64.81 Cr. A fall of more than about 7% QoQ fails the gate.
- Cash leg: H1 CFO/PAT above 0.5x. Receivables plus unbilled must not rise above the Mar-2026 level of about 122 days. The ageing tail must stay under 3% (tracker cash row). FY26 CFO/PAT was 0.33x. This leg can only be read off the H1 balance sheet and cash flow, which come with this filing set.
- Broken condition: revenue below ₹57 Cr or below $6.3m, with unbilled still above 20% of the quarter.
- "Two of three legs is not proof" (Notion, Mental Model).

The employee-cost row also gets its first test. Q1 employee cost rose 3.6% QoQ while revenue rose 13.6%. Falsifying: employee cost falling QoQ while revenue holds, which would mean the bench is being cut ahead of a client exit (tracker employee-cost row).

Tracker dates: the revenue, employee-cost and cash rows all carry a Next Check Date of 15-Nov-2026. The print is on 3-Nov. They should move to 4-Nov.

### Price: the re-engage line is crossed before the print

₹826 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:39 IST). 52-week ₹522-1,237. Market cap ₹1,287 Cr. On file: ₹5,714.60 pre-split, which is ₹1,143 post-split (Notion property and Key Notes, 21-Sep close). Against the record (my arithmetic, not filed):

- Down 28% from the ₹1,143 on file. Down 33% from the ₹1,237 52-week high, which must have been printed after 21-Sep.
- 6% below the bottom of the ₹880-900 entry zone. 18% above the ₹700 MoS. 31% above the ₹630 three-year Track 2 entry.
- 20.7x base FY27 EPS of ₹40. Ex-treasury (₹100/share on file), about 18.2x. That is just below the Track 1 RRM multiple of 18.7x. Price is about 3% below the Track 1 base fair value of ₹848.
- The 22-Sep page said "current PE above destination (25x) = stop-and-flag". That flag has cleared. The forward PE is now below the capped destination.

The rules on file say: "WATCHLIST on thesis, AVOID at price; re-engage on the Nov-2026 proof gate or a price inside the entry zone" (Notion, FY27 valuation). The price leg has fired. The gate leg reports in five days.

Nothing on file explains the fall. No TAAL filing appears in any run INDEX from 09-Oct to 28-Oct. The Sep-2026 SHP shows Mukul Agrawal unchanged at 8.92% and holders up 18% to 13,452 (tracker SHP row, SECONDARY). The 22-Sep page flagged a new retail base "that has never seen a bad quarter from this name" (Notion, Unasked). A 28% fall with no filing, ahead of results, looks like that base leaving early. Or someone is pricing a weak quarter. [INFERENCE]

### Two readings

- Bull: the business has not changed on file. The rupee stayed near 95.6 through Q2 (tracker USD/INR). Q1 margin was 32.6%. Proposing an interim dividend in the first results after a 1:5 split is not what a board expecting a bad quarter usually does [INFERENCE]. It also gives the first answer to the "cash utilisation, directors will take a call" line, which is graded MISS (Notion, promise ledger). Cash goes to shareholders instead of a treasury book that now has a ₹350 Cr limit.
- Bear: the top client is 24% of revenue and about 70% of FY23-FY26 growth (Notion, V3). Q1 might have been a bulge, not a new level. That question has been open since 10-Sep. Cash conversion was 0.33x in FY26. An interim dividend can be paid out of treasury, so it proves nothing about operating cash.
- The ONE observation that separates them: Q2 consolidated revenue on 3-Nov. At ₹60.2 Cr or more, with H1 CFO/PAT above 0.5x, the gate passes, and the price sits below an entry zone built on a base case the print supports. Below ₹57 Cr, or below $6.3m with unbilled above 20%, the gate breaks. Then the ₹880-900 zone itself is stale, because base EPS of ₹40 assumes Q1 +1% a quarter (Notion, Projections).

### What it does not say

- The dividend amount. With a ₹350 Cr Section 186 limit approved and no treasury policy (tracker treasury row), the payout size is the first number on capital allocation this management has filed. Read it against operating PAT, not reported PAT.
- Any call. The investor-call promise is graded MISS x6 (Notion, promise ledger). Syngene and Unimech, in this same batch, hold calls and post the audio within hours.
- "First" interim. I find no prior interim on file. Whether this is a new practice or a relabel cannot be told from this filing.

Nothing odd inside. Two working days of notice is met, and the record date gives the 7-day notice that Reg 42 asks for.

### Verdict

No thesis-broken trigger fires. "Kills it" needs the top client to slow, a treasury default, or the rupee back at 90, and none is observed. On your rules the price leg of re-engagement has fired before the gate leg. I read that as: the 3-Nov print decides, and it decides at a price inside your own zone. Whether a starter (ceiling SMALL, A25 only) is allowed before the gate reads, or only after it, is a ruling I do not find on file. That is your call.

## Cosmo First: Q2 board set as price nears ₹720 entry

Cosmo First is in COMPANIES MASTER as WATCHLIST, entry ₹720, MoS ₹575, position "Small (2-3%) when triggered: not now" (Notion, properties). The page has no Downstream Signals property, so no tracker rows are linked. The page was last edited 10-Jul-2026.

What the filing says [FILED]: the board meets on Tuesday 3-Nov-2026 to approve unaudited results for the quarter ended 30-Sep-2026 (Cosmo_First_Board_Meeting_Intimation_for_Approval_Of_d102c6.txt, p.1). It is signed by Jyoti Dixit, CS, at 15:06 IST on 28-Oct. That is six days' notice, well inside the Regulation 29 minimum. On its own it carries no information.

It matters for two reasons that are on file, not in the filing.

**1. The master decision gate has no logged result.** The page names Q1 FY27 results (August 2026) as "the master decision gate" (Notion, Master Decision Gate). It has three legs:
- US refund receipt confirmed or firm timing announced.
- Q1 capex under ₹25 Cr.
- Quarterly annualised ROCE above 14%.

The rule: all three fire, starter justified; two fire, stay WATCHLIST; none fire, downgrade to WATCHLIST/AVOID. No Q1 FY27 review, Key Notes entry or gate reading is on the page. The last Key Notes entry is the AR read of 10-Jul. So the gate passed with no recorded result. Whether the name is WATCHLIST on a pass, a split or a miss is NOT FOUND. The Q2 print on 3-Nov is the next chance to read all three legs, plus the AR-added items #11 (covenant variance) and #12 (Cosmo Global Films turnover and PAT).

**2. The price has walked to the buy line.** ₹735 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:36 IST). 52-week ₹561 to ₹996. Market cap ₹1,930 Cr. My arithmetic, not filed:
- 2.1% above the ₹720 entry. The action table says "BUY: CMP below ₹720" (Notion, Valuation Verdict). A 2% move triggers the price leg of the entry gate.
- 27.8% above the ₹575 MoS. The 52-week low of ₹561 is below the MoS, so the stock traded under MoS some time in the past year.
- 13.8% below the ₹853 on file (STALE, Notion on file 10-Jul-2026). Market cap fell from ₹2,239 Cr on file to ₹1,930 Cr. No Key Notes entry explains the fall.
- Trailing PE on FY26 PAT of ₹156 Cr (Notion, Valuation Verdict) is about 12.4x, against 14.4x on file. Against the ~15x destination PE, the PE gap is about 1.21x. It still fails the 1.3x hurdle, but by less than the 1.11x on file. That uses FY26 PAT; any Q1 change is not on file.

Note that the ₹720 entry was built on the ₹60.31 FY26 EPS growing 15% a year (Notion, Entry Price Calculation). If Q1 FY27 earnings fell, the ₹720 line itself is stale, and a price arriving at ₹720 is not the same as value arriving there.

**Two readings for 3-Nov.**
- Bull, with evidence on file: the capex cycle is done (commitments down from ₹122.59 Cr to ₹43.37 Cr, AR); specialty chemicals at 20% PAT margin; US tariff reversal with ₹60 Cr+ refund expected in 6-12 months [MGMT]; trade receivables ageing improved sharply. A falling price into a finished capex cycle is how a cyclical gets cheap.
- Bear, with evidence on file: 6-8 new BOPP lines in India including Uflex's 54,000 MT; management's own specialty mix dip to 50% in FY27; covenant variance on ₹559.91 Cr of term loans needed a waiver; sales tax incentive receivable up 43% with the auditor flagging state delays; Cosmo Global Films was loss-making at ₹(7.06) Cr. A 14% fall since July with no logged news fits a weak Q1.
- The one observation that separates them, on 3-Nov-2026: the 30-Sep balance sheet. Monitor #7 is dated to exactly this print: net debt below ₹1,050 Cr by Sep-2026 is green, above ₹1,150 Cr is red (Notion, Quarterly Monitoring Checklist). Read it with H1 capex. Against the FY27 guide of under ₹100 Cr, H1 above about ₹50 Cr is behind the guide (my arithmetic, pro-rata). Net debt above ₹1,150 Cr with H1 capex above ₹50 Cr is the bear case, whatever the price.

**What the filing does not say.** It mentions only the quarter, not the half year. A September quarter result must carry the half-year balance sheet and cash flow, so expect them on 3-Nov. It also says nothing on the trading window, which most intimations restate. Hygiene, not a flag.

**Cross-links.**
- Garware Hi-Tech (on file): its 26-Oct call said it has received about ₹31 Cr of US tariff refunds through its US subsidiary [MGMT] (prior run 2026-10-26_0330, T05). Cosmo Films Inc USA is a direct subsidiary with ₹419 Cr revenue (Notion, AR AOC-1). [INFERENCE] If a peer is already collecting refunds, Cosmo's gate leg 1 should be testable on 3-Nov. A Q2 silence on refunds would be informative.
- The same Garware call said the PPF anti-dumping petition was approved by Commerce but not by Finance [MGMT]. That removes a protection for Cosmo Consumer's PPF ramp. The BOPET duty on file is a different product and is not affected.

No thesis-broken trigger fires. The name moves toward a decision: price is at the entry line and the gate that was meant to decide it is unlogged. Before 3-Nov, the useful step is to log the Q1 FY27 gate result, or to rule that Q2 now stands as the gate. That is your call.

## Goodluck India: defence revenue disappears in Q2, steel margin slips

Status on file: Decision Status blank, Promoter Verdict blank, no position. Entry band ₹390-420 is in the 09-Sep body; the Entry Price Max and MoS properties are blank. Claude's standing recommendation (not set) is "do not buy, watch the money" (Notion, 01-Oct). Two tracker rows linked; I read both: T-G1 Excellent Fincap drawdown (Threatens, next check 30-Nov) and the GDAL/Green Energy swap ratio (Neutral, next check 14-Nov). Items 65 and 66 are the same 15-page PDF, word for word; I read it once, including the eight results images (pp.5-12).

Price: ₹551 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:37 IST), 52-week ₹305-615, market cap ₹5,498 Cr. On file ₹565 (23-Oct).

### The quarter in numbers [FILED]

| ₹ Cr | Q1 FY27 | Q2 FY27 | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Consolidated revenue from operations | 1,287.44 | 1,362.80 | p.6 |
| Standalone revenue from operations | 1,205.94 | 1,365.78 | p.5 |
| Consolidated minus standalone (defence proxy) | +81.50 | (2.98) | my arithmetic |
| Consolidated EBITDA (press release basis) | 139.66 | 127.49 | p.6, p.13 |
| Consolidated finance cost | 30.44 | 40.00 | p.6 |
| Consolidated PAT | 67.22 | 50.67 | p.6 |
| PAT to owners | 63.61 | 48.87 | p.6 |
| Non-controlling interest | 3.61 | 1.80 | p.6 |
| Standalone EBITDA margin, (PBT + finance + depreciation) / revenue | 9.16% | 8.24% | p.5, my arithmetic |

The press release leads with "Revenue +38%" YoY (p.13). QoQ, consolidated revenue rose 5.9% while standalone rose 13.3%. The defence line the record tracks went from about ₹81 Cr to about nil. The release confirms it: the defence order "is currently under production with the initial phase comprising trial runs, testing and validation", and revenue "is expected in the latter part of the current financial year" (p.13).

Method caveat: "consolidated minus standalone" was the basis on 09-Sep, when eliminations were negligible. If the parent now sells material to GDAL, intra-group eliminations would hide some GDAL revenue. NCI of ₹1.80 Cr implies GDAL still earned a profit of roughly ₹8-9 Cr [INFERENCE]. The revenue line does not explain that profit. GDAL's own numbers are not disclosed: Note 3 reports a single segment, steel products (p.9), although defence was 22% of group EBITDA in Q1 (Notion, 09-Sep).

### Watch items, leg by leg (Notion, section 11)

- **Item 3, defence revenue against the ₹80 Cr Q1 run-rate ("a flat Q2 is a warning"): fails, and by more than "flat".** The FY27 guide of ₹300-350 Cr now needs about ₹222-272 Cr in H2, ₹111-136 Cr a quarter, from a line in trial runs. The FY28 ₹500 Cr base needed about ₹125 Cr a quarter "starting Q2/Q3". Q2 missed it. My arithmetic.
- **Item 5, standalone steel margin: fails.** 8.24% against 9.17% logged for Q1 and about 9.7% for FY26. Standalone other expenses rose 19.2% QoQ against revenue up 13.3% (p.5). Management blames fuel and global freight [MGMT, p.13]. The record's question was whether the funding engine is weakening. This quarter says yes.
- **Item 4, leverage:**
  - Consolidated: gross debt ₹1,350.5 Cr against ₹1,119.5 Cr at March. Cash and bank ₹304 Cr against ₹49 Cr (p.7). Net debt is about ₹1,046 Cr against ₹1,070 Cr, about 2.0x annualised H1 EBITDA against 2.6x for FY26. My arithmetic. That eases only because the GDAL equity cash sits on the balance sheet.
  - Standalone: borrowings rose ₹228 Cr to ₹1,230 Cr. Standalone net debt is about 2.7x annualised H1 EBITDA against about 2.5x at March. Standalone H1 CFO was negative ₹37.7 Cr; inventories absorbed ₹181.8 Cr (p.8). My arithmetic.
  - So the steel business borrowed more while the defence subsidiary held the new equity.
- **Item 1, financial closure:** not mentioned. Consolidated H1 capex was ₹143.6 Cr (p.8). The new aerospace line (below) is placed "within the overall capital outlay previously approved".
- **Item 2, swap ratio:** still none (p.14: "detailed terms, structure and financial implications ... remain under evaluation"). Tracker not touched.

### T-G1 (Excellent Fincap)

Not fired and not cleared. The standalone cash flow shows loans only to subsidiaries: ₹81.15 Cr given and ₹42.35 Cr refunded (p.8). Standalone other current assets rose ₹115 Cr to ₹479.7 Cr. That is ₹99 Cr above the consolidated figure, consistent with intra-group loans to GDAL [INFERENCE]. Nothing names Excellent Fincap. The half-year Reg 23(9) RPT disclosure is not in this filing. If it is due with results (the 23-Oct entry flagged this to verify), its absence is itself a gap. Check the exchange feed.

### What is odd inside the filing

- **The equity raise is ₹30 Cr short in the cash flow.** The H1 consolidated cash flow shows "proceeds from issue of equity shares" of ₹245.90 Cr (p.8). The record logged the GDAL allotment of 27-Sep as 73,60,000 shares at ₹375 = ₹276 Cr. The ₹30.1 Cr gap is unexplained: money received after 30-Sep, or shares allotted against something other than cash. NCI on the balance sheet rose from ₹37.65 Cr to ₹138.04 Cr (p.7). The allottees are still not named.
- **Prior-period EPS not restated for the 2:1 bonus.** Q1 shows ₹19.13, Q2 FY26 ₹12.43 and FY26 ₹54.45, all on the old share count. H1 FY27 shows ₹11.28 on the new count (p.6). Ind AS 33 requires retrospective adjustment [INFERENCE on the standard]. On a like-for-like basis Q1 is about ₹6.38, so Q2's ₹4.90 is down 23%. My arithmetic.
- **Dating errors:** the press release is dated "28th August 2026" (p.13). The consolidated current-quarter column is headed "30.09.2025" (p.6). This adds to the AGM-notice drafting errors already logged.
- **Tax:** consolidated current tax of ₹9.56 Cr is below standalone's ₹13.85 Cr (pp.5-6). That implies a tax credit in a subsidiary. Not explained.

### The other board items

- **MoA objects amended:** defence and non-defence systems, plus hydrogen in all forms (p.1). Read with the 12-Oct in-principle merger, this prepares the parent to absorb GDAL's defence business and Green Energy's hydrogen business [INFERENCE]. It does not move the swap-ratio test.
- **Registered office moves from Delhi to Uttarakhand (p.1):** no reason given. It needs a shareholder special resolution and Regional Director approval.
- **GDAL adds aerospace components:** about ₹80 Cr invested and "expected benefits approximately ₹180 Cr" (p.4). Revenue, profit or period are not defined, so the ₹180 Cr cannot be tested. It sits "within the overall capital outlay previously approved", so it does not add to the debt case as filed.
- **Cost auditor:** Raj Kaushik & Associates fills a casual vacancy after the death of the existing cost auditor, who was appointed at the 30-Sep AGM (p.3). Routine.
- **Limited reviews:** both unmodified, by Sanjeev Anand & Associates (pp.10-12). The consolidation covers Goodluck Infrapower (wholly owned) and GDAL.

### Two readings

- **Bull:** H1 consolidated revenue +34% and EBITDA +38% (p.13). Q2 volume +18%. The defence gap is a changeover between orders, not lost business: the M1A4 qualification (12-Oct) plus the order now in trials should fill H2. GDAL holds about ₹255 Cr of new cash to fund the expansion without parent debt.
- **Bear:** this is a 38-42% margin business inside a 3-13% margin converter (Notion, section 1), and it produced roughly nothing in Q2. The steel margin fell about 90 bps. The parent's debt rose ₹228 Cr with negative operating cash flow. The record already shows 2 of 3 multi-year promises missed and the FY27 defence guide cut once. Missing ₹300-350 Cr would be the next miss.
- **The separating observation:** Q3 FY27 consolidated minus standalone revenue, at the Q3 results around early February 2027. ₹100 Cr or more says the H2 ramp is real. Below ₹50 Cr makes the FY27 guide unreachable and confirms the bear case.

### Fair value

FY27E on file is PAT to shareholders ₹240 Cr, EPS ₹24 (Notion, section 6). H1 owner PAT is ₹112.5 Cr (EPS ₹11.28). H2 needs ₹127.5 Cr against Q2's ₹48.9 Cr a quarter. Defence has to return in Q3 for the estimate to hold. My arithmetic.

At ₹551 the stock is:
- 31.2% above the ₹420 entry top;
- 23.6% above the ₹4,450 Cr SoTP, which is still not redone at 69.08%;
- 24.4x annualised H1 EPS and 28.1x annualised Q2 EPS.

My arithmetic. Price does not invite action.

### Verdict

There is no formal thesis-broken list on file. Watch items 3 and 5 both fail this quarter. T-G1 and the swap ratio are both still untested. "Do not buy, watch the money" stands, and the defence ramp now has to be proven again from zero. That is your call.

₹ Cr, converted from lakhs (Fabtech_Tech_Outcome_Of_Board_Meeting_d6222b.pdf, p.5, p.7):

| Line | Q2FY27 | Q1FY27 | Q2FY26 | H1FY27 | H1FY26 |

Q2 revenue fell 24.2% and H1 fell 11.8% YoY. Q2 PAT fell 46.5% (my arithmetic).

### The monitoring checklist, leg by leg (Notion, 4-Aug workup)

- **Item 5, other income below 30% of PBT: FAIL.** Other income is 46.3% of H1 PBT before exceptional items (₹8.62 Cr of ₹18.60 Cr). Add the TSA gain and non-operating items are 62.9% of H1 PBT of ₹26.96 Cr (my arithmetic).
- **Item 4, order book above ₹900 Cr and book-to-bill above 1.2x: fails or depends.** The release says "~900 Cr" (press release p.2) [MGMT], the same "near Rs 900 Cr" as at the 4-Aug workup. A flat book after ₹167 Cr of H1 revenue implies H1 intake of about ₹167 Cr, a book-to-bill near 1.0x. That is my arithmetic, and it assumes the August figure was the opening book. The ₹800-850 Cr from Africa and Saudi Arabia is "under discussion", not booked.
- **Item 6, overdue receivables: not testable, as expected.** Gross receivables rose to ₹234.84 Cr from ₹211.46 Cr at Mar-26 and ₹158.46 Cr at Sep-25 (segment annexure, p.11). Ageing is not given.
- **Item 2, IPO-cash deployment: deployed, but into working capital.** ₹25.56 Cr of the working-capital object was used in Q2, ₹71.78 Cr in total. ₹125.75 Cr is still unused, including the full ₹30 Cr for acquisitions (p.10). The thesis wanted deployment to lift operating ROCE. In H1, it funded a cash outflow.

### Where the cash went

- Other current assets rose ₹38.74 Cr, from ₹35.19 Cr to ₹73.93 Cr (p.6, p.7). That is the single largest outflow, and the filing does not say what it is.
- Receivables rose ₹19.07 Cr.
- Trade payables fell ₹25.05 Cr.
- Customer-side other current liabilities rose ₹23.71 Cr.
- Borrowings rose ₹11.09 Cr, to ₹54.68 Cr.
- Cash plus bank deposits stayed flat at about ₹208 Cr. The ₹11.78 Cr of TSA proceeds and the new debt covered the operating outflow (p.6-7; my arithmetic).

By geography (p.11): Saudi receivables rose to ₹85.72 Cr from ₹63.37 Cr, against H1 Saudi revenue of ₹46.23 Cr. UAE receivables are ₹41.19 Cr against H1 UAE revenue of ₹19.65 Cr. These are gross segment figures before inter-segment eliminations. The release says "Maximum of our orders / projects are covered with LCs, leaving little room for receivable risk" [MGMT]. Receivables rising while revenue fell, the 248 days, and the ₹58.9 Cr over 365 days at FY26 (auditor key audit matter, Notion) do not fit that statement. If the orders are LC-backed, the LCs are not being drawn.

### What the release does not say

- It shows "EBITDA" of ₹15.57 Cr (16.0%) and a PAT margin of 16.3% (press release p.2). The EBITDA includes ₹5.45 Cr of other income, and PAT includes the ₹8.35 Cr exceptional gain. Neither is mentioned.
- It gives no cash flow.
- It says deferred orders were "delayed in timing, with no cancellations" [MGMT], with no value.
- It says "UAE operations demonstrated strong order traction in Q2" [MGMT], while UAE segment revenue fell 81%, from ₹47.18 Cr to ₹9.05 Cr (p.11). Traction may mean orders, not revenue. Either way, no number is given.
- The "8-9% operational PAT margin" for FY27 [MGMT] compares with H1 operating PBT of ₹9.98 Cr. That excludes other income and the exceptional gain, and comes to 6.0% of revenue before tax (my arithmetic).

### TSA: the record's inference was wrong

On 24-Oct I suggested the lower tranche-2 price might cause a Q2 write-down (Notion, 24-Oct block). The filing shows the opposite: a ₹8.35 Cr consolidated gain (₹12.32 Cr standalone), booked as an exceptional item in Q2 (p.9 note 6; p.17 note 6). The carrying value was the ₹5.63 Cr asset held for sale at Mar-26 (p.6). ₹13.99 Cr less ₹5.63 Cr is ₹8.35 Cr, which matches exactly (my arithmetic).

The sale completed on 30-Sep, not the 5-Oct expected in the 30-Sep filing (Notion, 01-Oct review). The cash landed in H1 investing flows, not Q3. Proceeds received were ₹11.78 Cr against consideration of ₹13.99 Cr (p.7). The ₹2.21 Cr difference is not explained. Tax withheld at source is possible [INFERENCE]. The stake detail also corrects the record: tranche 1 was 17% and tranche 2 was 16.33% of TSA, out of a 33.33% holding (p.9). The record's "~16% slice" was the tranche-2 piece only.

### Two readings

- Bull: Execution is back-loaded. H1FY26 was 46% of FY26 revenue. Q2 revenue rose 23% on Q1, and the Q2 operating margin of 11.0% cleared the 10% line. The release calls the security, standby and freight costs non-recurring [MGMT]. Cash of ₹208 Cr is intact, and ₹55 Cr of the IPO working-capital money is unspent. Morocco, Kenya and India grew.
- Bear: H1 revenue fell 12%. CFO was minus ₹41 Cr. ₹58 Cr went into receivables and unexplained other current assets in six months. 63% of H1 PBT is non-operating. Orders are being deferred. Borrowings are rising beside a cash pile. The largest subsidiary (UAE, H1 revenue ₹68.52 Cr and PAT ₹8.43 Cr) was reviewed by another auditor, not the group auditor (p.4).
- What separates them: to match FY26 revenue, H2 must reach ₹243.70 Cr, against ₹221.28 Cr in H2FY26 (my arithmetic). The Q3 FY27 results are due by mid-February 2027. Q3 revenue at or above about ₹122 Cr (half that H2) keeps the bull case alive. Below it, the bear case holds.

### Fair value

The ₹150.2 base FV is 13x FY27 EPS, which implies FY27 EPS of about ₹11.55, or PAT of about ₹51.3 Cr on 4.445 Cr shares (Notion, 4-Aug; my arithmetic). With H1 PAT at ₹19.24 Cr, including the one-off gain, H2 needs about ₹32.1 Cr. H2FY26 earned ₹16.38 Cr (my arithmetic). The base looks out of reach. The devil's-advocate operating-only range of ₹105-133 and the RRM value of ₹98.2 (Notion, 4-Aug) now look like the honest anchors. The price sits inside that range.

### Smaller items

- **Alan Dias, VP-HR, named Senior Management Personnel with immediate effect** (p.20-21). He has 24 years in HR. Routine.
- **The board was scheduled for 4:00 pm and started at 6:05 pm** (p.19).
- **The Executive Director signed the results from "Place: Iraq"** (p.10, p.18). Iraq is not on the release's list of regional operations (press release p.3) [FILED; significance unknown].
- **A new step-down subsidiary in Saudi Arabia.** A shareholders' agreement was signed, alongside Egypt (p.10 note 8c). No capital is disclosed. This is a third MENA entity on top of a cash outflow, and it is the jurisdiction with the largest receivables.

### Price

₹125 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:36 IST). 52-week range ₹115-258; market cap ₹556 Cr. My arithmetic: 1.55x the ₹80.6 zone top, which needs a further fall of about 35.5%. The price is 4.2% above the ₹120 on file (Notion, 24-Oct) and 8.7% above the 52-week low.

### Verdict

The entry conjunction on file needs all three of: price in ₹62.9-80.6, CFO/PAT above 30% on documented IPO-cash deployment, and promoter CONCERN resolved. All three fail, and the cash leg failed by a wide margin. No thesis-broken trigger applies to an unheld AVOID. The print moves the name further from a look, and FLAG-CASH STRUCTURAL now has a filed half-year behind it. AVOID on valuation stands, and Decision Status stays blank. That is your call.

## Insolation Ener: ₹224 Cr incentive is real, but slow and accrual-shaped

Insolation is in COMPANIES MASTER. Decision Status is not set on the page; the 11-Sep Halt 1 note says rules imply WATCHLIST / SHALLOW WATCH and leaves the status for you. Position Size reads "None. Starter 2-3% only on Trigger 1 (ageing below 20%)". Promoter Verdict CONCERN, size ceiling Small (Notion, properties). Entry is trigger-gated, not price-gated: "Price is not a trigger" (Notion, Re-engage triggers). Last Key Notes: 29-Sep board outcome (Usha Sharma, former Rajasthan Chief Secretary, appointed independent director) and 11-Sep Halt 1 rework. Four tracker rows are linked; all four were read.

**What the filing says [FILED]** (Insolation_Ener_Disclosure_Under_Regulation_30_Of_The_SE_870448.pdf):
- Wholly owned subsidiary Insolation Green Energy Pvt Ltd (IGEPL) received a RIPS-2022 Turnover Linked Incentive (TLI) Entitlement Certificate dated 26-Oct-2026 for the Sawarda, Jaipur module plant (p.1).
- Eligible fixed capital investment ₹179.12 Cr, approved by the State Level Screening Committee. TLI rate including boosters 1.98% of eligible turnover (p.1).
- Ceiling up to 125% of eligible investment, so up to about ₹223.90 Cr, "subject to ... ceilings and disbursement requirements" (p.2). 179.12 × 1.25 = 223.90, which checks (my arithmetic).
- Claimed yearly on eligible turnover, realised over "approximately Nine years" (p.2).
- 100% electricity duty exemption, estimated above ₹1 Cr a year (p.2).
- Phase 2: the company is "pursuing" about ₹185 Cr of subsidy and incentives, documents submitted, "hopeful" of approval "in coming quarters" (p.2) [MGMT].
- Accounting: will be recognised "in accordance with the applicable accounting standards" (p.3). No timing, no line item.

**The arithmetic (my arithmetic, not filed).**
- ₹223.90 Cr over about 9 years is about ₹24.9 Cr a year on average.
- At 1.98%, ₹24.9 Cr a year needs about ₹1,257 Cr of eligible turnover a year.
- FY26 revenue was ₹2,146 Cr, modules 82% (Notion, Business narrative), and FY27 base is ₹3,000 Cr (Notion, Projections). If all of that were eligible, 1.98% would pay ₹42 Cr to ₹59 Cr a year and the cap would be hit in 4 to 5 years, not 9.
- So the 9-year horizon implies the eligible base is well below group turnover. Likely only Sawarda output, net of resale and the older Jaipur plant [INFERENCE]. The filing does not state the eligible turnover base or any annual cap.
- Scale: ₹24.9 Cr a year is about 83 bps of margin on the ₹3,000 Cr FY27 base, about 18% of FY27 base owners' PAT of ₹135 Cr, and about 10% of FY28 base ₹241 Cr, all pre-tax. Undiscounted, the full ₹223.90 Cr is about 14% of today's ₹1,572 Cr market cap; discounting over nine years makes it smaller.

**What it touches on file.**
- The FY28 margin bridge (11.5% to 15%) carries no incentive line (Notion, Projections). The projections ignore this. It is a genuine upward input to every scenario, of under 1 point of margin.
- The core concern on file is cash, not profit: FY26 PAT up 59% with operating cash minus ₹73 Cr; receivables 39.5% past 6 months (Notion, Business narrative; tracker row "receivables ageing"). A state incentive booked on accrual adds profit before it adds cash. If disbursement lags, it adds a government receivable to a book that already fails the cash test [INFERENCE]. The filing gives no disbursement timing.
- Kill trigger 4 (CARE debt/EBITDA above 4x; tracker row "leverage"). Base peak debt ₹1,500 Cr on FY27 base EBITDA ₹345 Cr is 4.3x (Notion). Add the full ₹24.9 Cr a year to EBITDA and it is ₹1,500 / ₹370 Cr = 4.05x. Still above 4x. My arithmetic. The incentive does not clear the leverage problem.
- Where the money lands: IGEPL. The page puts the cell plant and the ₹1,134 Cr IREDA loan in IGEPL too (Notion, Business narrative), and its unasked item 2 is IREDA escrow trapping project cash in IGEPL while the loss-making standalone parent carries the guarantees. This incentive feeds the same box, not the parent.
- Open item partly answered: the page asks which subsidiary holds the aged receivables, "IGEPL (modules) or IIPL (EPC)". This filing confirms IGEPL owns the Sawarda module plant. It does not say where the ageing sits.

**Gate test, leg by leg.**
- Trigger 1 (Sep-26 receivables past 6 months below 20%): not touched. Next check 15-Nov (tracker).
- Trigger 2 (cell COD, then 14% margin): not touched. Narmadapuram is in Madhya Pradesh, so RIPS does not apply to it.
- Trigger 3 (LLPs, land title, EPS numerator, ARS sequence): not touched. Still open; next check 03-Nov (tracker).
- Kill triggers: not touched (see 4.05x above).
- The margin tests on file need a ruling. The FY27 separating observation is Q2 EBITDA margin: above 13% means bull is live, below 11% means base is generous (Notion, Projections). Trigger 2's add leg is one quarter at 14% or more. A TLI accrual can be worth about 0.8 points. If the 26-Oct certificate is booked in the Q2 accounts (a post-period event before results approval) or as a catch-up in Q3, it can move a quarter across those lines without any change in the business [INFERENCE]. Please rule: are the margin gates measured excluding TLI income?

**Two readings.**
- Bull: a filed, state-approved entitlement, not a promise. It cuts net module cost by about 2% of eligible turnover for nine years, when module prices are under pressure (Notion, margin bridge: price pressure minus 100 to 200 bps). Phase 2 could add ₹185 Cr more.
- Bear: it is small per year, back-ended by turnover, and paid by a state on its own schedule. It flatters accrual profit while the open question is cash. Phase 2 is a hope with no scheme, state or plant named. The board added a former Rajasthan Chief Secretary on 28-Sep and the certificate followed on 26-Oct. The SLSC approval date is not given, so the sequence proves nothing either way. But it means future Rajasthan approvals will be read against that appointment [INFERENCE].
- The observation that separates them: the H1 FY27 results (mid-Nov). Look for (a) where TLI income is booked and how much in Q2, and (b) any incentive receivable in the balance sheet or ageing notes. Cash received, or an accrual under ₹7 Cr for the quarter with no new receivable line, supports the bull. A catch-up accrual above ₹7 Cr with nothing received supports the bear. ₹7 Cr is one quarter of the ₹24.9 Cr average plus rounding; the threshold is mine.

**What it does not say.**
- The eligible turnover base, any annual cap, and the start date of the nine-year window. Is it retrospective to Sawarda's start?
- Disbursement mechanics and timing.
- Which state, scheme and plant the Phase 2 ₹185 Cr is for, and what investment it rests on.
- Whether any TLI was already accrued in FY26 or Q1 FY27 in anticipation.

**Inconsistencies.** "We wish to informed you" (p.1). The text says "the Company is also entitled" when the entitlement is the subsidiary's (p.1). Hygiene only.

**Price.** ₹71.3 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:37 IST). 52-week range ₹62.0 to ₹186.0; market cap ₹1,572 Cr.
- Down 22.1% from ₹91.56 on file (STALE, Notion on file, NSE close 07-Sep-2026). 15% above the 52-week low. My arithmetic.
- At ₹71.3: FY27 base EPS ₹6.1 is 11.7x; FY27 bear ₹2.8 is 25x; FY28 base ₹10.9 is 6.5x (Notion, Projections; PE is my arithmetic).
- No price lines are on file. Entry is trigger-gated, so the fall does not invite action by your rules.

**Verdict.** No thesis-broken or kill trigger fires. The filing adds a real but small profit stream that the projections lack. It does nothing for the three things that gate this name: receivables ageing, the cell COD, and the integrity items. SHALLOW WATCH stands on the rules on file. That is your call.

## The others, briefly

### Arisinfra Solu.: fourth subsidiary gets parent guarantee, results land before votes

Arisinfra is in COMPANIES MASTER as HELD, Medium 4% (Notion, Position Size: "entered ₹106 on 09-Jul-2026"). The Avg Cost property is still blank. One tracker row is linked: "ARISINFRA: AUSPL scheme explanatory statement". I read it. Its falsifying observation was already tested and passed on 24-Oct (Notion, 24-Oct entry). Today's filings do not touch it.

Two filings on 28-Oct.

Item 19, the guarantee [FILED] (Arisinfra_Solu_Disclosure_Under_Regulation_30_Of_Sebi_L_7ec9d7.pdf):
- Arisinfra gives a corporate guarantee "up to" ₹3.90 Cr, including interest and other charges, for White Roots Infra Pvt Ltd's credit facility from State Bank of India, Wagle Industrial Estate, Thane (p.2).
- Event date 28-Oct-2026, 12:00 noon (p.1). Signed by the WTD and CFO at 18:14 the same day (p.1). That is inside the 24-hour window.
- Promoters have "no interest". The guarantee is called "arm's length" (p.2).
- Impact line: a contingent liability, and "at this point, there is no impact" (p.2).

Item 20, the board meeting [FILED] (Arisinfra_Solu_Board_Meeting_Intimation_for_Considering_01223d.pdf, p.1): board on Tuesday 3-Nov-2026 for Q2 and H1 standalone and consolidated results. The trading window has been shut since the 24-Sep letter.

Tested against the record:

- Size. ₹3.90 Cr is about 0.5% of ASL standalone net worth of about ₹725 Cr at 30-Jun (Notion, 24-Oct entry). My arithmetic, not filed. On its own it is immaterial.
- The channel. It is not immaterial as a pattern. The 24-Oct entry logged guarantees outstanding of ₹84-154 Cr: Stride NCDs for BIPL (₹60 Cr or ₹130 Cr, the notice contradicts itself), Lionheart ₹20 Cr, and BIPL's ₹4 Cr Unity SFB line. Add ₹3.90 Cr and the range is about ₹88-158 Cr, against ₹12.30 Cr of quantified contingent liabilities at FY26 year end (Notion, AR Step 5). My arithmetic. White Roots is the fourth subsidiary to get parent credit support, after BIPL, Lionheart and JS Infra Core.
- White Roots is a blank on file. Notion names it only once, in the FY26 subsidiary list (Notion, AR Step 1). There is no stake, no business line, no revenue, no net worth. The filing does not add any of these. If White Roots is not wholly owned, this is another case of the parent carrying credit risk for a minority it does not name. That is the JS Infra pattern (70%, minority unnamed) [INFERENCE].
- Which approval covers it. The postal ballot open now (e-voting 26-Oct to 24-Nov) seeks guarantee authority for BIPL and JS Infra only (Notion, 24-Oct entry). White Roots is not in it. The guarantee most likely sits under the general ₹2,000 Cr borrowing, lending and security envelope approved at the 31-Jul AGM, Items 4-7 (Notion, AR Step 8) [INFERENCE]. The filing does not say which approval it uses, or the board date.
- One point in the other direction. The lender is SBI, a scheduled bank. The 29-Sep entry read the unrated private-credit NCDs (Lionheart, Stride) as a sign that bank lines were tight [INFERENCE on file]. A plain SBI line at a subsidiary cuts slightly against that reading.

What it does not say that it should:
- White Roots' shareholding, business, revenue, PAT or net worth.
- The facility type and amount. "Up to ₹3.90 Cr including interest" implies a smaller principal.
- Tenor, security, and any guarantee commission. "Arm's length" with no commission figure cannot be checked.
- The approving resolution and the board date.

Internal inconsistency: the annexure says "Rs 3,90,00,000 Crores (Rupees Three Crores Ninety Lakhs Only)" (p.2). Read literally, that is ₹3.9 lakh crore. The words and the summary make clear it is ₹3.90 Cr. This is a drafting slip, not a flag. It fits the drafting errors already logged (the Stride ₹60 Cr against ₹130 Cr, and BIPL D/E of -0.04).

Gates:
- Thesis-broken triggers: none fire. Debtor days, the over-6-month bucket, margin and a discounted equity raise are all untouched.
- Pre-committed trim rule (debtor days over 150, or the over-6-month bucket over ₹90 Cr, means trim to 2%): still untested. The consolidated Q1 number is still not on the page. The 3-Nov board meeting brings the first consolidated balance sheet since FY26. The H1 balance sheet is mandatory with Q2 results.
- The rule was written for "Q1 FY27" (Notion, Position Action Log). Please rule on whether the 30-Sep H1 print counts as its test. Without that ruling, the one rule on file for this position has no live test date.

The two readings for the H1 print, from the 24-Oct entry, still hold:
- Bear: consolidated debtor days above 150, or standalone loans to subsidiaries above about ₹250 Cr (₹195.10 Cr at 30-Jun). The Q1 standalone proxy was about 170 days (my arithmetic, on file).
- Bull: debtor days under 130 with subsidiary loans flat.
- Add one leg: the H1 contingent-liabilities and guarantees figure, if the results disclose it, against the ₹88-158 Cr range above.

Timing matters. Results come on 3-Nov. The postal ballot closes 24-Nov and the AUSPL scheme meeting is 25-Nov. You will have the H1 balance sheet before both votes.

Price: ₹141 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:35 IST); 52-week ₹82.2-175; mcap ₹1,157 Cr.
- Up from ₹131 on file (24-Oct). Above the ">₹130 avoid zone" (Notion, Recommendation). 33% above your ₹106 cost. Above the ₹110 entry and ₹88 MoS.
- ₹1,157 Cr / ₹52.70 Cr FY26 PAT-to-parent = about 22.0x. That is at the 22x sector cap and above the 16.5x destination PE. My arithmetic.

Verdict: no trigger fires. The guarantee is small, but it is the fourth subsidiary-credit item in a month, and the subsidiary is not described anywhere on file. It adds a little weight to the open 29-Sep exit-or-cut-to-Small recommendation without settling it. The H1 print on 3-Nov is the observation that will. That is your call.

### Indiamart Inter.: one-on-one investor call while #6 ruling waits

IndiaMART is in COMPANIES MASTER as HELD, "Small (2%: trimmed from 3% post Q4 FY26)", cost ₹2,015, add line ₹1,439, MoS ₹1,151 (Notion, properties). No new trigger fires today. HELD 2% is unchanged in Notion. The #6 ruling is the outstanding decision. That is your call.

The cheese leg may be met on management's word, but no filed board approval is on file. The New Age leg has no rupee figure on file. So the Q2 print tests a binary the file never scored for Q1.

Price: ₹239 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:38 IST). 52-week range ₹178-377. Market cap ₹3,005 Cr. This move matters more than the filing:
- Down 12% from ₹271.65 on file (Trendlyne, 1-Oct-2026), and down 21% from ₹303 in the 09-Oct broker note. My arithmetic.
- Now below the ~₹259 haircut Base FV (₹305 less 15%), which is still a pending ruling (Notion, 03-Oct). It is 8.6% above your ₹220 cost, 53% above Entry ₹156, and well below the ₹305 trim line.

At ₹239 that branch is now out of reach before the print. Whether to finish the 4%→2.5% trim at a lower price is a different question from the one you logged.

Which tests this print carries. 3-Nov is the first print after the Q2 master gate failed to confirm (Notion, Q2 concall overlay, Section 2: 1 answered, 1 partial, 2 dodged). The record set three things to press at Q3 CY26 (Notion, concall overlay 5B):

The Q2 review shows two unmodified opinions, so this is the existing practice [FILED pattern, Notion Step 6C]. A modified opinion on 3-Nov would hit the "material audit qualification" thesis-broken leg directly.

Two readings, one observation.

- What separates them: organic constant-currency growth stated in the Q3 CY26 release or call, above 5% (bull) or below 3% (bear), dated 3-Nov-2026 or the call after it.

### Uniparts India: 04-Nov H1 print tests both gate legs

Uniparts India is in COMPANIES MASTER. The Decision Status property is blank. The 08-Sep AR review logged "WATCHLIST, route to full workup" in Key Notes and page body (Notion, Key Notes 08-Sep; AR Verdict). There is no position, no Entry Price Max, no MoS and no Current Market Price on file. No valuation has run. The page has no Downstream Signals property, so no tracker rows are linked; the AR review says none exist yet (Notion, Step 10.5).

What it says [FILED] (Uniparts_India_Board_Meeting_Intimation_for_Discussion__45e053.pdf, p.1):
- Board meets Wednesday 04-Nov-2026 to approve standalone and consolidated unaudited results for the quarter and half year ended 30-Sep-2026.
- Trading window closed from 01-Oct-2026, stays closed to 06-Nov-2026.
- Dated 28-Oct-2026, signed by Jatin Mahajan, Company Secretary, 17:52 IST.

Routine notice, but it dates the one test on file. Every review since August names a single clean metric for this print: H1 FY27 CFO/PAT, read with core operating PBT ex-other-income margin. Bull: CFO/PAT at or above 1.0x and margin at or above 19%. Bear: CFO/PAT below 0.8x or margin below 17% (Notion, Step 8, AR Step 13-W, AGM note).

One wording point first. The page calls it "audited H1 FY27 CFO/PAT" in five places. The filing says "unaudited". Half-year results carry a limited review, not an audit. The 08-Sep AR entry already uses the audited FY26 figure (1.10x) to say the Q1 scare is refuted. The H1 cash flow on 04-Nov will be limited-reviewed only. Please rule whether a limited-review H1 cash flow counts for the gate, or whether the cash leg waits for the audited FY27 accounts.

The tests, leg by leg. All my arithmetic on Notion figures, not filed:
- Cash leg. Q1 operating cash was ₹44 Cr on PAT ₹56.61 Cr, about 0.77x, spoken on the call and unaudited (Notion, Step 5). Management guides Q2 in line with Q1 (Notion, Section B Step 2). If Q2 PAT is near ₹57 Cr, H1 PAT is about ₹113 Cr.
  - Bull at 1.0x needs H1 CFO of about ₹113 Cr. So Q2 alone must bring about ₹69 Cr, about 1.2x of a Q1-sized PAT.
  - Bear below 0.8x means H1 CFO under about ₹91 Cr, so Q2 under about ₹47 Cr.
  - Between those, the leg is neither bull nor bear. Q2 cash about equal to Q1's ₹44 Cr lands H1 near 0.78x, just inside bear.
- Margin leg. Q1 core PBT ex-OI was ₹66.74 Cr on revenue ₹347.38 Cr, 19.2% (Notion, Step 1A). That clears 19% by only 0.2 points. Two items on file can take it below the bear line:
  - FX. About ₹6.8 Cr of unquantified FX benefit sat in Q1 gross profit (Notion, Step 4). Without it, Q1 core margin is about 17.3%.
  - Material cost. Q1 was 33.3% of revenue against the company's own 34-37% band and FY26's 36.3% (Notion, AR Step 3). If Q2 material cost returns to the FY26 level, about 3 points come off margin. That takes a Q1-shaped quarter to about 16.2%, below the 17% bear line.
  - So the margin leg rests on whether Q1's FX and low material cost repeat. The page already flagged both as not durable (Notion, Combined Verdict flags).

Two readings:
- Bull: FY26 audited CFO/PAT was 1.10x and 1.44x over two years. The Q1 dip was growth-funded working capital (Notion, AR Step 4). H2 is guided above H1, so H1 working capital should not unwind against the business. Q2 near Q1 revenue with no fresh inventory build gives Q2 cash above Q1.
- Bear: working capital is 136-139 days and structural (Notion, AR Step 5). Mexico deliveries start in Q3 (Notion, M2). A new warehouse needs stock before it ships, and that stock would sit in the 30-Sep balance sheet [INFERENCE]. Margin was flattered by FX and the warehouse mix is at the top of its 52-56% band (Notion, M15, M16).
- The observation that separates them: on 04-Nov, H1 CFO above about ₹113 Cr with core margin at or above 19% confirms bull. H1 CFO below about ₹91 Cr, or core margin below 17%, confirms bear. The inventory line in the H1 balance sheet will show whether Mexico stocking explains a miss.

What the notice does not say: no earnings call date. Q1 had a call (Notion, Section B). That call is where the two must-ask items on file come back: the unanswered diversification-volatility question and the refused FX number (Notion, Step 8.5 Q15, Q16).

One loose end on file: the 29-Sep AGM note says voting results were "not yet filed". Nothing in this run closes that. Under Reg 44(3) they are due within two working days of the 28-Sep AGM [INFERENCE on the rule's timing]. Most likely filed and not logged, but it is unchecked.

Price: ₹719 (screener.in, 29-Oct-2026 05:40 IST); 52-week ₹400 to ₹916; market cap ₹3,245 Cr. No price is on file, so there is no move to measure. Market cap over price gives about 4.51 Cr shares, which ties to FY26 PAT ₹158.32 Cr over EPS ₹35.07. On TTM EPS ₹39.97 (Notion, Step 6) that is about 18.0x trailing. My arithmetic. No Entry, MoS, stop or trim line exists, so there is nothing to test the price against until the full workup runs.

Verdict: no thesis exists, so no thesis-broken trigger can fire. The filing dates the gate to 04-Nov. The margin leg starts only 0.2 points above its bull line, with two named tailwinds that may not repeat. The name stays a watchlist name waiting for full workup. That is your call.
